A deterministic LLM-judge overlay bounds evaluator noise, and a 60-run negative-class smoke pilot shows the reward signal is not drop-in training-safe: restraint criteria pass at 0.929 prevalence, a gameability an eval harness can tolerate but a training reward cannot. We scaffold coupling to a Megatron+SGLang+GRPO loop per Corecraft Section 5.2 and leave training-reward ablations as future work. Environment, tasks, rubrics, and harness are released under Apache 2.0.

</details>


### [74] [SepsisAI Orchestrator: A Containerized and Scalable Platform for Deploying AI Models and Real-Time Monitoring in Early Sepsis Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22331)
*Santiago Ospitia,John Sanabria,John Garcia-Henao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SepsisAI-Orchestrator 是一个开源部署平台，旨在解决临床败血症预测模型的工程落地难题，并通过负载测试量化了临床 AI 推理在 Kubernetes 环境下的“U型”扩缩容瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床 AI 模型虽在预测性能上表现优异，但在实际临床落地的过程中面临异构数据、缺乏标准化部署流程以及与医院环境要求的计算时延/并发不匹配等系统级瓶颈。

Method: 提出 SepsisAI-Orchestrator 平台，架构包括：(1) 基于 HL7 FHIR 的 CDA 数据预处理；(2) NoSQL 存储；(3) 容器化 LightGBM 模型及 REST API 服务；(4) Streamlit 可视化仪表盘。使用 Docker 和 Kubernetes 进行编排，并利用 k6 工具在 50-1000 并发用户规模下进行负载测试。

Result: 在 12 线程 CPU 环境下，将副本数从 3 个增加到 12 个，p95 时延从 3.3s 降低至 1.41s（降幅 57.3%），并消除了请求失败；过量配置（24 或 48 个副本）会导致调度冲突并造成性能退化。

Conclusion: SepsisAI-Orchestrator 通过合理的容器编排能有效解决临床 AI 部署中的时延与并发问题。研究发现，推理服务的副本数与主机 CPU 线程数的匹配程度至关重要，存在“U型”性能缩放特性，过高的副本数会导致调度竞争进而降低性能。

Abstract: Despite strong predictive results in the clinical machine learning literature, the translation of these models into bedside use remains limited by systems-level barriers: heterogeneous data representations, the absence of standardized deployment workflows, and a mismatch between research prototypes and the concurrency and latency requirements of hospital environments. We present the SepsisAI-Orchestrator, an open-source modular platform that addresses this deployment gap for early sepsis detection. The platform integrates HL7 FHIR-inspired Clinical Document Architecture (CDA) preprocessing, NoSQL storage, a containerized LightGBM classifier served via REST APIs, and a Streamlit clinical dashboard, orchestrated with Docker and Kubernetes. A previously validated LightGBM model (F1 0.87-0.94 on PhysioNet 2019) is reused without modification; the contribution lies in the surrounding infrastructure and its empirical characterization under load. Using k6 with 50-1000 concurrent virtual users, we find that replica count must be matched to the physical CPU thread count of the host: scaling from 3 to 12 replicas on a 12-thread CPU reduces p95 latency from 3.3s to 1.41s (57.3% reduction) and eliminates all request failures, while over-provisioning to 24 or 48 replicas degrades performance due to scheduler contention. To our knowledge this U-shaped scaling behavior has not been quantified previously for clinical AI inference workloads. We do not claim prospective clinical validation. Source code and deployment manifests are available at https://github.com/nucleusai/sepsisai-orchestrator.

</details>


### [75] [Lumberjack: Better Differentially Private Random Forests through Heavy Hitter Detection in Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22756)
*Christian Janos Lebeda,David Erb,Tudor Cebere,Aurélien Bellet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Lumberjack 是一种新型差分隐私随机森林算法，通过构建深度决策树并结合创新的分层数据重击检测算法与激进的剪枝技术，显著提升了隐私保护下的模型效用，刷新了该领域的效能表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的差分隐私随机森林算法通常会严重牺牲模型性能（效用），导致其在处理敏感表格数据时缺乏实用性。目标是设计一种算法，能在差分隐私约束下实现更好的隐私-效用权衡。

Method: 提出了一种名为 Lumberjack 的差分隐私随机森林算法。核心技术包括：1. 构建大规模随机决策树；2. 引入一种新型针对分层数据的 ($\varepsilon, δ$)-DP 重击检测算法（Heavy Hitter Detection），其误差缩放为 $O_{\varepsilon,δ}(\sqrt{\log h})$；3. 应用激进的隐私保护剪枝策略，仅保留数据分布充足的节点。

Result: 在基准数据集上的实证评估表明，Lumberjack 始终优于现有的差分隐私随机森林方法，在实际隐私预算下实现了更高的预测准确性，达到了该领域的领先水平。

Conclusion: 通过构建大规模随机决策树并结合创新的隐私保护剪枝算法，Lumberjack 在保持严格差分隐私约束的前提下，显著提升了随机森林的性能，证明了差分隐私随机森林在实际应用中具有极高的可行性与潜力。

Abstract: Random forests are widely used in fields involving sensitive tabular data, but existing approaches to enforcing differential privacy (DP) typically degrade performance to the point of impracticality. In this paper, we introduce Lumberjack, a differentially private random forest algorithm that achieves substantially higher utility by constructing large random decision trees and then applying aggressive, privacy-preserving pruning to retain only sufficiently populated nodes. A key component of our approach is a novel $(\varepsilon,δ)$-DP heavy hitter detection algorithm for hierarchical data, whose error is $O_{\varepsilon,δ}(\sqrt{\log h})$ for trees of height $h$ and may be of independent interest. This favorable scaling enables the use of significantly deeper trees than in prior work, leading to improved expressiveness under privacy constraints. Our empirical evaluation on benchmark datasets shows that Lumberjack consistently outperforms prior DP random forest methods, establishing a new state of the art. In particular, our approach yields substantial improvements in the privacy-utility trade-off for practical privacy budgets. Our findings suggest that carefully designed DP random forests can close much of the utility gap, highlighting a promising and underexplored direction for future research.

</details>


### [76] [A Reproducible Log-Driven AutoML Framework for Interpretable Pipeline Optimization in Healthcare Risk Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21528)
*Rui Huang,Lican Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了 yvsoucom-iterkit 框架，通过日志驱动的自动化架构揭示了疾病预测管道中关键组件的贡献度与冗余性，证明专注于核心组件可显著提升预测模型的效率与稳健性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对疾病风险预测中存在的特征异质性、样本有限和严重的类别不平衡问题，旨在提高模型构建的准确性、过程的可复现性和组件交互的可解释性。

Method: 提出一种确定性且日志驱动的自动化机器学习框架 yvsoucom-iterkit，通过配置级系统记录管道，结合随机森林重要性分析、组件相似性评估及跨种子鲁棒性测试，对超过 18,000 种管道配置进行挖掘。

Result: 验证了搜索空间的冗余性，明确了数据增强（0.454）、模型选择（0.198）和不平衡处理（0.101）是影响 Pima 数据集的主要因素；集成模型在两类任务中展现了优异的表现（Pima: 0.89 F1, Stroke: 0.94 F1）及更强的跨种子鲁棒性。

Conclusion: AutoML的优化可以聚焦于一小部分高影响力的核心组件，通过分析组件的归因和相互作用，能够更高效地构建稳健的疾病风险预测模型。

Abstract: Accurate and reproducible disease risk prediction remains challenging due to heterogeneous features, limited samples, and severe class imbalance. This study introduces yvsoucom-iterkit, a deterministic and log-driven automated machine learning framework that formulates pipeline optimization as a fully reproducible, configuration-level system. Each pipeline is encoded as a traceable log entity, enabling analysis of component attribution, interactions, similarity, and cross-seed robustness. Experiments on the Pima Indians Diabetes and Stroke datasets across more than 18,000 pipeline configurations reveal a structured and partially redundant search space, where performance is governed by a small subset of interacting components. Random Forest importance analysis identifies augmentation (0.454), model choice (0.198), and imbalance handling (0.101) as key drivers on Pima, while imbalance handling dominates Stroke (0.406). Component similarity analysis shows strong redundancy, with feature selection variants (biMax-biMean) exhibiting low RMS distance (0.0252), mixup closely matching no augmentation (0.0279), and TomekLinks aligning with no imbalance handling (0.0325), whereas Gaussian noise shows greater divergence from no augmentation (0.10). The framework achieves strong and stable performance using ensemble models (Weighted-F1 0.89, Macro-F1 0.88 on Pima; Weighted-F1 0.94 on Stroke), while Macro-F1 remains lower on Stroke (0.67) due to class imbalance. Cross-seed analysis reveals a performance-robustness trade-off, with ensembles showing lower variability (0.023-0.026) than SVM. These results indicate that effective AutoML optimization can focus on a reduced set of high-impact components.

</details>


### [77] [DualOptim+: Bridging Shared and Decoupled Optimizer States for Better Machine Unlearning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21539)
*Xuyang Zhong,Qizhang Li,Yiwen Guo,Chen Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DualOptim+ 是一种新型的大语言模型遗忘优化框架，通过将状态拆分为基础和增量部分并利用自适应策略处理梯度冲突，有效平衡了遗忘效果与模型效能，并支持低功耗的 8bit 量化运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机器遗忘方法在大语言模型中往往难以兼顾“有效遗忘目标数据”与“保持模型在保留数据上的性能”之间的平衡，且面临计算资源消耗大的挑战。

Method: 提出了一种新型优化框架 DualOptim+，其核心在于将模型状态拆分为“基础状态”（捕捉共有表示）和“增量状态”（保留特定目标的残差），并根据遗忘与保留梯度之间的方向冲突自适应调整两种状态。此外，还引入了 8bit 量化版本以降低内存开销。

Result: 在虚构与真实世界的遗忘任务、安全对齐以及多任务学习中，DualOptim+ 展现出了优于现有方法的性能平衡，且其 8bit 变体在显著降低内存需求的同时保持了高性能。

Conclusion: DualOptim+ 能够在大语言模型的机器遗忘任务中，在遗忘效果、保留性能和计算效率之间实现更优的权衡，提供了一种高效且可扩展的优化方案。

Abstract: We propose DualOptim+, a novel optimization framework for improving machine unlearning in large language models. It introduces a base state to capture common representations shared by forgetting and retaining objectives and delta states to preserve objective-specific residuals. This architecture allows the optimizer to adaptively bridge shared and decoupled states based on the directional conflict between forgetting and retaining gradients. We further introduce DualOptim+ 8bit, a quantized variant that reduces memory overhead without compromising performance. Extensive experiments across fictitious and real-world unlearning, safety alignment, and multi-task learning tasks demonstrate that DualOptim+ consistently achieves a superior trade-off between different objectives. Codes are available at https://github.com/CityU-MLO/DualOptimPlus.

</details>


### [78] [Provable Joint Decontamination for Benchmarking Multiple Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21543)
*Zhenlong Liu,Hao Zeng,Hongxin Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对大语言模型基准数据污染问题，本文提出了 JECS 框架，通过引入保形预测方法，在多模型评估中实现了可证明的全局污染率控制，有效克服了传统检测方法在公平性与准确性上的缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基准数据去污染方法要么缺乏理论保证（基于评分的方法），要么在多模型场景下因独立检测导致基准不统一，难以进行公平比较。因此需要一种能够在大规模多模型评估中提供全局控制的方法。

Method: 提出“联合包络保形选择”（Joint Envelope Conformal Selection, JECS）。通过计算每个模型的保形 p 值，取其逐项最大值，并基于阈值以上的右尾观察构建最大 p 值零分布的保守包络，进而应用自适应 Benjamini-Hochberg (BH) 程序进行选择。

Result: 实验表明，JECS 在保持设定的全局污染率（GCR）控制的同时，相比传统的 max-p 基准方法表现出了更高的检测效能（power）。

Conclusion: JECS 是一种有效且稳健的多模型基准去污染框架，通过“联合包络保形选择”方法，在保证全局污染率控制的前提下，提升了检测性能。

Abstract: Benchmark data contamination has become a central challenge in LLM evaluation: when evaluation examples appear in the training data of one or more audited models, reported performance can be inflated and cross-model comparisons become unreliable. A broad line of training-data detection work designs scores to quantify how strongly a model memorizes a given data point, but these score-based methods lack theoretical guarantees. Recent conformal approaches provide provable false-identification control for a single model; however, applying them separately to each model can produce model-specific benchmarks, undermining fair comparison across models. In this work, we formalize multi-model benchmark decontamination as a joint selection problem and propose Joint Envelope Conformal Selection (JECS), a conformal procedure that enables global contamination rate (GCR) control under stated assumptions. Specifically, JECS computes per-model conformal p-values, aggregates them by the per-item maximum, and reconstructs a conservative envelope of the max-p null distribution from right-tail observations above a data-driven threshold. By applying the adaptive Benjamini-Hochberg (BH) procedure to the envelope-rescaled values, we select a benchmark with provable GCR control. Extensive experiments across various models and benchmarks demonstrate that JECS achieves higher power than the max-p baseline while consistently maintaining the target GCR control.

</details>


### [79] [Expectation Consistency Loss: Rethink Confidence Calibration under Covariate Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21552)
*Jinzong Dong,Zhaohui Jiang,Bo Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对协变量偏移下模型置信度校准困难的问题，本文提出了期望一致性条件及相应的 ECL 损失函数，实现了高效、稳定的无监督领域自适应置信度校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统置信度校准假设训练数据与测试数据独立同分布（IID），在协变量偏移下效果不佳；现有方法处理协变量偏移时往往依赖不稳定的重要性加权，且在类级或规范校准方面存在局限。

Method: 1. 推导了置信度校准在协变量偏移下的充要条件——期望一致性条件（Expectation consistency condition）。2. 设计了无监督领域自适应损失函数——期望一致性损失（ECL），支持多种校准指标（类级、正则、最高标签）。3. 证明了 ECL 的样本复杂度与 ECE 一致，并提出了基于小批量的训练方案。

Result: 在模拟数据集和真实世界协变量偏移数据集上的实验结果表明，该方法能够有效且稳定地校准目标域的预测置信度，优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了期望一致性（EC）条件，证明了协变量偏移不一定导致置信度失准，并通过 ECL 损失函数提供了一种无需标签、理论完备且易于训练的校准方案，有效解决了协变量偏移下的置信度校准难题。

Abstract: Confidence calibration for classification models is vital in safety-critical decision-making scenarios and has received extensive attention. General confidence calibration methods assume training and test data are independent and identically distributed, limiting their effectiveness under covariate shifts. Previous calibration methods under covariate shift struggle with class-wise or canonical calibrations and often rely on unstable importance weighting when density ratios are large or unbounded. Given the above limitations, this paper rethinks confidence calibration under covariate shifts. First, we derive a necessary and sufficient condition for confidence calibration under covariate shifts, named Expectation consistency condition, which reveals covariate shifts do not necessarily lead to uncalibrated confidence and provides a weaker condition for confidence calibration than global covariate distribution alignment. Then, utilizing Expectation consistency condition, this paper proposes an unsupervised domain adaptation loss to calibrate confidence of the target domain, named Expectation consistency loss (ECL), which is compatible with canonical calibration, class-wise calibration, and top-label calibration. Third, we prove that computing ECL loss has the same sample complexity as Expected Calibration Error (ECE) and provide a theoretically grounded mini-batch trainable scheme for ECL loss. Finally, we validate the effectiveness of our method on both simulated and real-world covariate shift datasets.

</details>


### [80] [Tabular foundation models for robust calibration of near-infrared chemical sensing data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21544)
*Robin Reiter,Denis Cornet,Fabien Michel,Lauriane Rouan,Gregory Beurier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究评估了TabPFN在近红外光谱分析中的应用潜力，结果显示TabPFN在中小型校准场景下优于传统方法，但建议与领域特定先验知识及不确定性评估策略结合使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近红外光谱（NIR）技术虽广泛应用，但其校准模型在处理高维共线性数据、样本量小、预处理依赖性及异常值干扰等方面仍面临挑战，亟需评估表格基础模型（Tabular Foundation Models）作为新校准策略的潜力。

Method: 在66个近红外光谱（NIR）数据集（54个回归、12个分类）上，对比了TabPFN与传统方法（PLS/PLS-DA、Ridge回归）、集成模型（CatBoost）及深度学习模型（1D-CNN）的性能，并采用统一的验证框架，严格区分预处理与模型选择阶段。

Result: 在回归任务中，预处理优化的TabPFN表现最优；在分类任务中，直接使用原始光谱的TabPFN平均排名最高。研究同时指出，在面对光谱异常值和外推样本时，传统化学计量学模型仍具竞争力。

Conclusion: 表格基础模型（TabPFN）可以作为现有化学计量学方法在近红外光谱分析中的有力补充，特别是在中小型数据集上表现出色，但针对异常值处理和外推场景仍有改进空间。

Abstract: Near-infrared spectroscopy is increasingly used as a rapid, non-destructive chemical sensing technology for the analysis of food, pharmaceutical, biological, and environmental samples. However, the practical deployment of NIR sensors still depends on calibration models able to handle high-dimensional, collinear spectra, limited sample sizes, preprocessing dependence, spectral outliers, and extrapolation beyond the calibration domain. Here, we evaluate whether tabular foundation models can provide a new calibration strategy for NIR chemical sensing. We benchmark TabPFN on 66 NIR datasets covering 54 regression and 12 classification tasks, and compare direct inference on raw spectra with preprocessing-optimized inference against PLS/PLS-DA, Ridge, Catboost, and one-dimensional convolutional neural networks. The study uses a unified validation framework in which preprocessing and model selection are performed exclusively on calibration data before external test evaluation. In regression, preprocessing-optimized TabPFN achieves the best overall average rank and significantly outperforms PLS, CatBoost, TabPFN on raw spectra, and CNN-1D, while remaining statistically comparable to Ridge. In classification, TabPFN applied directly to raw spectra provides the best average rank, with performance close to the optimized variant. Robustness analyses show that TabPFN provides strong average predictive performance but that its advantage decreases on spectral outliers and extrapolated samples, where classical chemometric models remain competitive. These results suggest that tabular foundation models can complement established chemometric workflows for NIR chemical sensing, especially in small- to medium-sized calibration settings, while highlighting the need for spectroscopy-specific priors and uncertainty-aware deployment strategies.

</details>


### [81] [On the Sample Complexity of Discounted Reinforcement Learning with Optimized Certainty Equivalents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21763)
*Oliver Mortensen,Mohammad Sadegh Talebi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了递归OCE风险敏感强化学习的样本复杂度，证明了其可PAC学习的条件及对应的理论界限，显著提升了CVaR风险度量下的样本效率分析精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨有限折扣MDP中风险敏感强化学习的PAC学习性，明确哪些OCE风险衡量标准是可学习的，并建立其对应的样本复杂度理论框架。

Method: 采用基于生成模型的模型化（model-based）方法，通过分析递归OCE目标的数学性质，推导出PAC样本复杂度上界，并结合信息论方法构建下界。

Result: 1. 给出了OCE目标为PAC可学习的效用函数充要条件；2. 证明了若效用函数定义域不为实数全集，则问题不可PAC学习；3. 在紧致性上，推导出了关于状态-动作空间大小(SA)的紧致下界；4. 针对CVaR_τ，将样本复杂度的τ依赖项优化至1/τ²。

Conclusion: 本文界定了风险敏感型强化学习中优化确定性等价（OCE）目标的可PAC学习性边界，证明了当效用函数域不是全实数域时问题不可PAC学习，并为值学习和策略学习提供了紧致的样本复杂度下界（针对CVaR_τ的依赖关系进行了重要改进）。

Abstract: We study risk-sensitive reinforcement learning in finite discounted MDPs, where a generative model of the MDP is assumed to be available. We consider a family or risk measures called the optimized certainty equivalent (OCE), which includes important risk measures such as entropic risk, CVaR, and mean-variance. Our focus is on the sample complexities of learning the optimal state-action value function (value learning) and an optimal policy (policy learning) under recursive OCE. We provide an exact characterization of utility functions $u$ for which the corresponding OCE defines an objective that is PAC-learnable. We analyze a simple model-based approach and derive PAC sample complexity bounds. We establish that whenever $u$ does not have full domain $\text{dom}(u)\neq \mathbb{R}$, the corresponding problem is not PAC-learnable. Finally, we establish corresponding lower bounds for both value and policy learning, demonstrating tightness in the size $SA$ of state-action space, and for a more restricted class of utilities, we derive lower bounds that makes the dependence on the effective horizon $\frac{1}{1-γ}$ explicit. Specifically, for $\text{CVaR}_τ$ we show that the correct dependence on $τ$ is $\frac{1}{τ^2}$, thus improving by a factor of $\frac{1}τ$ over state-of-the-art although our bound has a suboptimal dependence on $\frac{1}{1-γ}$.

</details>


### [82] [MMD-Balls as Credal Sets: A PAC-Bayesian Framework for Epistemic Uncertainty in Test-Time Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21783)
*Ahanaf Hasan Ariq*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文为测试时自适应（TTA）提供了一个基于PAC-Bayesian和非精确概率论的理论框架，量化了分布偏移下的预测不确定性，并建立了首个将MMD偏移量与模型泛化性能挂钩的严谨理论边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的测试时自适应（TTA）方法虽然能提升分布偏移下的模型性能，但缺乏将分布偏移程度与预测可靠性联系起来的数学形式化保证。

Method: 1. 利用最大均值差异（MMD）对源分布与目标分布之间的偏移进行参数化；2. 使用PAC-Bayesian框架推导基于MMD的泛化界；3. 将源分布周围的MMD球解释为Walley非精确概率论中的“信度集”（credal sets）以量化认识论不确定性；4. 在RKHS-Lipschitz损失假设下建立边界，并分析测地线（geodesic）保持属性。

Result: 1. 推导出了基于MMD偏移惩罚的PAC-Bayesian泛化边界；2. 获得了通过MMD浓度不等式表示的有限样本版本；3. 建立了针对信度集中所有分布的统一最坏情况风险界，并实现了风险的上下界分解；4. 通过测地线保持边界解释了核引导自适应保护局部特征几何结构的机制。

Conclusion: 该研究通过PAC-Bayesian框架与非精确概率论（credal sets）的结合，解决了测试时自适应（TTA）中缺乏预测可靠性保证的问题，提出了一种能够区分认识论不确定性与偶然不确定性、并为何时进行适配提供决策准则的理论框架。

Abstract: Test-time adaptation (TTA) methods improve model performance under distribution shift but lack formal guarantees connecting shift magnitude to prediction reliability. We develop a PAC-Bayesian framework yielding generalization bounds explicitly parameterized by the maximum mean discrepancy (MMD) between source and target distributions. Our principal contribution is interpreting MMD-balls around the source distribution as credal sets in Walley's imprecise probability theory, yielding natural epistemic uncertainty quantification. We establish: (i) a PAC-Bayesian bound with an MMD-dependent shift penalty under an RKHS-Lipschitz loss assumption; (ii) a finite-sample version via MMD concentration; (iii) a uniform worst-case risk bound over all distributions in the credal set, with a lower-upper risk decomposition; and (iv) geodesic preservation bounds explaining why kernel-guided adaptation protects local feature geometry. The credal set interpretation separates epistemic from aleatoric uncertainty and provides a principled decision criterion for when adaptation is warranted.

</details>


### [83] [Beyond Single Slot: Joint Optimization for Multi-Slot Guaranteed Display Advertising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21556)
*Zhaoqi Zhang,Jiaming Deng,Miao Xie,Linyou Cai,Qianlong Xie,Xingxing Wang,Siqiang Luo,Gao Cong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对多广告位保证型展示广告分配问题，本文提出了一种联合优化框架，通过离线二分图匹配与约束机制，显著提升了广告变现效率、ROI及合约执行稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GD广告分配方法多基于“单广告位”假设，无法对多广告位页面进行全局优化，导致存在广告位冗余、合约执行不平衡及曝光过于集中等问题。

Method: 提出多广告位GD（保证型展示广告）联合优化框架；将分配问题建模为离线二分图匹配问题；引入“合约轮盘”机制处理广告位排他性，以及“PV约束”实现曝光控制；采用可扩展的分配优化算法以适应大规模部署。

Result: 在美团广告平台实测中，ARPU提升了28.99%（70%流量下），合同稳定性与履行鲁棒性显著增强。

Conclusion: 该联合优化框架有效解决了多广告位分配中的冗余、失衡与集中度问题，在保障合约完成率的同时显著提升了广告主的ROI与平台的变现效率。

Abstract: Guaranteed display advertising is crucial for platform monetization, yet existing methods often operate under a single-slot assumption, limiting their ability to optimize allocation across multi-slot page views. In this paper, we propose a novel joint optimization framework for multi-slot GD allocation, addressing key challenges such as slot-level redundancy, contract imbalance, and exposure concentration. Our approach formulates the allocation as an offline bipartite matching problem with a contract roulette mechanism for slot exclusivity and Page View constraints for impression control, and incorporates a scalable allocation optimization algorithm for efficient large-scale deployment. Extensive online tests on the Meituan advertising platform demonstrate that our method significantly improves merchant ROI, platform revenue efficiency, and contract fulfillment robustness. Specifically, online A/B tests show a 28.99% increase in Average Revenue Per User under 70% traffic, and DID analysis further indicates improved contract stability, demonstrating the strong applicability and effectiveness of our framework in real-world advertising deployments.

</details>


### [84] [Three Costs of Amortizing Gaussian Process Inference with Neural Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21798)
*Robin Young*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立了神经过程（LNP）逼近高斯过程（GP）的理论框架，定量分析了通过摊销推断引入的KL散度误差来源，并提出了优化模型架构配置（如二阶池化）以提升预测性能的具体策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经过程通过摊销推断将$O(n^3)$精确GP计算降至$O(n)$，但目前缺乏对其近似质量来源及规模化限制的理论刻画。研究旨在量化这种近似带来的误差并指导架构设计。

Method: 使用KL散度（Kullback--Leibler divergence）对GP与LNP预测分布之间的差异进行定性与定量的分解与界定，并推导了各误差项随表示维度 $d$ 和样本量 $n$ 的缩放行为。

Result: 推导出了三个误差项的缩放特性：瓶颈项随表示维度$d$呈指数或多项式衰减（取决于核函数）；标签污染项通常为$O(1)$，仅观测噪声部分随$n$衰减；摊销误差源于共享编码器。这些结果直接定量了模型架构规模与任务复杂性之间的关系。

Conclusion: 研究通过理论分析揭示了神经过程（LNP）在近似高斯过程（GP）推断时的三个误差源（标签污染、信息瓶颈、摊销误差）。研究结果表明，增加表示维度可有效缓解瓶颈误差，但标签污染误差具有持续性。基于此，提出了通过仅基于位置信息估计方差以及使用二阶池化（second-order pooling）来减少摊销误差的架构改进建议。

Abstract: Neural processes amortize Gaussian process inference, replacing the exact $O(n^3)$ posterior with a learned $O(n)$ map from context sets to predictive distributions. For a class of latent neural processes, we bound the Kullback--Leibler (KL) divergence between the GP and LNP predictives, decomposing it into three interpretable sources, namely label contamination as the neural process uses label values to estimate a quantity that is label-independent in the exact GP, an information bottleneck because the finite-dimensional representation cannot resolve the full context geometry, and amortization error from a single encoder network shared across all contexts. The bottleneck truncation term decays in the representation dimension $d$ as $O(e^{-cd^{2/d_x}})$ for squared-exponential kernels on $\mathbb{R}^{d_x}$ where $c > 0$ is a kernel-dependent constant and as $O(d^{-2ν/d_x})$ for Matérn-$ν$ kernels, directly linking architecture sizing to kernel smoothness and input dimension. The label contamination term is $O(1)$ in general, with only the observation-noise component decaying as $O(1/n)$, identifying a persistent cost of routing uncertainty estimation through a label-dependent representation. These results characterize the costs of amortization within the analyzed class and yield architectural recommendations to predict variance from context locations alone in the GP-amortization regime, and replace mean aggregation with second-order pooling to close the dominant amortization gap.

</details>


### [85] [Aerodynamic force reconstruction using physics-informed Gaussian processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22111)
*Gledson Rodrigo Tondo,Igor Kavrakov,Guido Morgenthal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种概率物理信息机器学习方法，通过不确定性量化和物理约束，实现了从含噪动力响应数据中准确反演气动载荷，在复杂结构分析中具有广泛应用价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有气动力模型存在过度简化，且在面对噪声或不完整观测数据时，模型验证面临巨大挑战，难以准确预测复杂结构系统的气动响应。

Method: 提出了一种概率物理信息机器学习（Probabilistic Physics-Informed Machine Learning）方法，该方法无需正则化方案，能够处理异构和多保真度数据，并通过结构动力响应的测量值重建气动力。

Result: 以大贝尔特东桥（Great Belt East Bridge）为对象进行的仿真验证表明，该方法在均方根误差、幅值、相位角及峰值等方面均与真实载荷高度吻合。

Conclusion: 该方法通过概率物理信息机器学习实现了对噪声/不完整数据的强鲁棒性，在气动力反演中展现出高精度，为模型验证、未来载荷预测及结构健康监测提供了通用且有效的工具。

Abstract: Accurate modeling of aerodynamic loads is essential for understanding and predicting the responses of complex structural systems. However, these models often rely on simplifications of the true physical forces, introducing assumptions that can limit their accuracy. Validating such models becomes particularly challenging in the presence of noisy or incomplete data. To address this, we introduce a probabilistic physics-informed machine learning approach designed to reconstruct the underlying aerodynamic loads from noisy measurements of structural dynamic responses. The model avoids overfitting, eliminates the need for regularization schemes, and allows for the use of heterogeneous and multi-fidelity data during the training process. The efficacy of the approach is demonstrated through the reconstruction of aerodynamic loads on the Great Belt East Bridge, simulated under a linear unsteady assumption. Results show a strong agreement between true and predicted loads, particularly related to root mean squared errors, magnitude, phase angle and peak values of the signals. The method for load reconstructing holds broad applicability, such as modeling validation, future load estimation, and structural damage prognosis.

</details>


### [86] [From Sequential Nodes to GPU Batches: Parallel Branch and Bound for Optimal $k$-Sparse GLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22188)
*Jiachang Liu,Andrea Lodi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对离散组合优化问题，本文提出了一种能够批量处理BnB节点的CPU-GPU协作框架，通过自定义内核和数据填充解决了异构节点处理和数据通信延迟问题，实现了显著加速并支持高质量的模型分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前一阶优化方法在连续优化领域表现优异，但在涉及离散变量、组合结构及非线性目标函数的问题（如基数约束广义线性模型）中，由于BnB算法节点异构性导致的序列处理瓶颈及CPU-GPU间的频繁数据通信，计算效率低下。

Method: 提出了一种通用且模块化的CPU-GPU协作框架，通过在GPU上批量处理多个BnB节点，利用填充（padding）技术和轻量级自定义内核处理不规则的数据结构，显著减少了数据传输开销并提升了并行计算效率。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法在处理具有挑战性的实例时实现了1至2个数量级的加速，且能保证零最优性间隙（zero optimality gap），同时支持Rashomon集的收集以进行下游统计分析。

Conclusion: 该研究所提出的CPU-GPU框架证明了通过批量处理和特定核函数优化，可以有效解决分支定界（BnB）算法在处理离散、组合优化问题时的效率瓶颈，并实现了性能的数量级提升。

Abstract: GPUs have significantly accelerated first-order methods for large-scale optimization, especially in continuous optimization. However, this success has not transferred cleanly to problems with discrete variables, combinatorial structure, and nonlinear objectives, such as certifying optimal solutions for cardinality-constrained generalized linear models. Major challenges include the sequential processing of heterogeneous nodes in branch and bound (BnB) and frequent data movement between the CPU and GPU. We propose a simple, generic, and modular CPU--GPU framework that processes multiple BnB nodes in batches on GPUs. The framework is built around a small set of GPU-efficient routines and uses padding together with lightweight custom kernels to handle irregular node data structures. Experiments show one to two orders of magnitude speedups and zero optimality gap on challenging instances. The framework can also be extended to collect the entire Rashomon set, enabling downstream statistical analysis such as variable-importance analysis and model selection under secondary user-specific measures (e.g., AUC in classification).

</details>


### [87] [Objective-Induced Bias and Search Dynamics in Multiobjective Unsupervised Feature Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21561)
*Mathieu Cherpitel,Thomas Bäck,Martijn R. Tannemaat,Anna V. Kononova*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究分析了影响无监督特征选择的多种核心因素，发现不同的评价指标与正则化方向会导致截然不同的搜索行为；其中，PCA重构损失被证明是一种比轮廓系数更有效且具备高预测能力的无监督特征评价指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无监督特征选择通常被建模为优化子集质量和大小的多目标问题，但当前的各种评估目标、正则化策略和初始化策略对最终结果的影响尚不明确。此研究旨在探究这些核心因素如何影响特征选择的搜索动力学及性能表现。

Method: 在受控实验环境中，使用具有已知信息性、冗余性和无关特征的合成数据集，对比了6种组合配方。这些配方涵盖了三种评价指标（准确率、轮廓系数、PCA重构损失）与两种子集大小正则化方向（最小化或最大化）的组合，并分析了搜索动态和Pareto最优前沿的质量。

Result: PCA重构损失函数生成的子集在测试准确率上可与监督特征选择相媲美；相比之下，基于轮廓系数的选择方法倾向于产生平凡的低基数子集，且在预测性能上的表现较弱。

Conclusion: 目标函数的设计对于有效的多目标无监督特征选择至关重要。研究表明，PCA重构损失函数表现优异，能产生紧凑且具高预测价值的特征子集，而轮廓系数（Silhouette score）则存在明显的偏差问题。

Abstract: Unsupervised feature selection is commonly formulated as a multiobjective optimisation problem that jointly optimises subset quality and subset size. Yet the behaviour of this formulation depends critically on the choice of evaluation objective, the direction of subset-size regularisation, and the initialisation strategy. We study these factors in a controlled setting using a synthetic dataset with known informative, redundant, and irrelevant feature types. Six formulations are compared by combining three evaluation objectives: accuracy, silhouette score, and PCA reconstruction loss with subset-size minimisation or maximisation. The results show that formulation strongly affects both search dynamics and the quality of the resulting Pareto front. Silhouette-based formulations exhibit a strong bias toward trivial low-cardinality solutions and remain weak proxies for predictive performance. In contrast, the proposed PCA loss objective produces compact subsets with test accuracy comparable to subsets obtained by directly optimising supervised accuracy. Overall, the study shows that objective design is central to effective multiobjective unsupervised feature selection.

</details>


### [88] [Generative Modeling by Value-Driven Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22507)
*Pablo Moreno-Muñoz,Adrian Müller,Gergely Neu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于离散随机控制的价值驱动传输（VDT）模型，通过将生成任务转化为线性规划，实现了高效、直线型的测度传输，并在多种生成任务上表现出超越传统流和扩散模型的优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对现有生成模型（如流、扩散模型或薛定谔桥）在传输路径、计算效率及实现复杂性上的局限，提出一种更具理论基础和实际效率的生成建模框架。

Method: 基于离散时间随机控制的测度传输框架，将其转化为线性规划（LP）问题，通过对偶变量求解最优价值函数，并开发了一种免模拟（simulation-free）的原始-对偶算法来计算近似最优价值函数和对应的传输策略。

Result: 实验结果证明，VDT策略能够实现笔直的传输路径，推断速度快且鲁棒性强，同时易于集成条件生成、无分类器引导和非配对数据翻译等功能。

Conclusion: VDT框架提供了一种高效、鲁棒且计算简便的生成建模方法，能够获得直线传输路径，优于现有的扩散模型和流模型，在多个任务中表现出强大的性能和扩展潜力。

Abstract: We propose a new framework for generative modeling based on a discrete-time stochastic control formulation of measure transport. Adapting classic results from control theory, we formulate our problem as a linear program whose dual variables correspond to the \emph{optimal value function} of the control problem, which directly encodes the optimal control policy. Exploiting this LP formulation, we develop an efficient simulation-free primal-dual algorithm for computing approximately optimal value functions and the associated \emph{value-driven transport} (VDT) policies which approximate the true optimal policy. We show that well-trained VDT policies enjoy numerous favorable properties in comparison with other state-of-the-art methods based on flows, diffusions, or Schrödinger bridges: they lead to straight transport paths which can be simulated quickly and robustly, and can be enhanced in all the same ways as diffusion and flow-based models (e.g., conditional generation, classifier-free guidance, unpaired data-to-data translation are all easy to incorporate). We evaluate our methodology in a range of experiments, with results that indicate strong performance and good potential for scalability.

</details>


### [89] [Leveraging Self-Paced Curriculum Learning for Enhanced Modality Balance in Multimodal Conversational Emotion Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21565)
*Phuong-Anh Nguyen,The-Son Le,Duc-Trong Le,Cam-Van Thi Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对多模态对话情绪识别中的不平衡学习问题，本文提出了一种基于自步课程学习（SPCL）的即插即用框架，通过动态难度调度显著提升了模型性能与稳健性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MERC任务中存在模态不对齐（modality misalignment）及不平衡学习（imbalanced learning）挑战，限制了多模态信息的有效融合与利用。

Method: 提出了一种基于自步课程学习（SPCL）的框架，通过双层难度衡量器（评估话语级与对话级难度）和学习调度器，动态引导模型从易到难进行训练，以缓解模态不平衡并增强稳健性。

Result: 在IEMOCAP和MELD数据集上进行了广泛实验，验证了该方法对多种架构和模态设置的有效性，其中IEMOCAP上的加权F1分数提升了约1.2%至6.6%，MELD上提升幅度最高达10.4%。

Conclusion: 本文提出的基于SPCL的即插即用框架有效解决了多模态对话情绪识别中的模态不对齐和不平衡学习问题，证明了该方法作为轻量级模块具有优异的通用性和性能提升效果。

Abstract: Multimodal Emotion Recognition in Conversations (MERC) is a crucial task for understanding human interactions, where multimodal approaches integrating language, facial expressions, and vocal tone have achieved significant progress. However, modality misalignment and imbalanced learning remain major challenges, limiting the effective utilization of multimodal information. To address this issue, we propose a plug-and-play framework based on Self-Paced Curriculum Learning (SPCL) for MERC. We introduce a dual-level Difficulty Measurer that captures both utterance-level and conversation-level challenges. The utterance-level score models fine-grained modality-specific difficulty, while the conversation-level score captures broader dialogue structures, including emotional dependencies and modality coherence. Based on these scores, the Learning Scheduler dynamically guides training from easier to more difficult instances. By integrating SPCL into existing MERC architectures, our method alleviates modality imbalance and improves model robustness. Extensive experiments on the IEMOCAP and MELD datasets demonstrate consistent improvements across different architectures and modality settings. On IEMOCAP, SPCL improves weighted F1-score by approximately +1.2% to +6.6% over baseline models, while on MELD, gains reach up to +10.4%. These results highlight the effectiveness and generalizability of SPCL as a lightweight plug-and-play module for multimodal emotion recognition.

</details>


### [90] [Multiple Neural Operators Achieve Near-Optimal Rates for Multi-Task Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22724)
*Adrien Weihs,Hayden Schaeffer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究证明了多任务算子学习（MNO）在共享表示下具有与单任务学习相同的缩放定律，且多任务扩展架构在复杂性上具有渐近等价性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在多任务学习设置下，学习一组算子（如 MNO 架构）的近似复杂度和统计统计复杂度，验证共享表示在算子学习中的效率。

Method: 利用 Lipschitz 函数类对算子映射进行刻画；通过数学分析推导近似误差与统计泛化误差的上界；构建下界以证明极大极小（minimax）收敛率。

Result: 推导出了近乎最优的近似和泛化误差上界；揭示了“参数复杂度诅咒”；证明了多任务算子学习遵循与单任务学习相同的缩放定律。

Conclusion: 多任务算子学习的缩放定律与单任务算子学习一致；共享表示不会增加整体复杂度；MNO 架构与输入拼接式的 DeepONet 扩展在最坏情况下的渐近复杂度等价。

Abstract: We study the approximation and statistical complexity of learning collections of operators in a shared multi-task setting, with a focus on the Multiple Neural Operators (MNO) architecture. For broad classes of Lipschitz multiple operator maps, we derive near-optimal upper bounds for approximation and statistical generalization. On the lower-bound side, we establish a curse of parametric complexity and prove corresponding minimax rates. Together, these results show that shared representations across tasks do not increase the overall cost: multi-task operator learning follows the same scaling laws as single operator learning. We also compare MNO with a multi-task extension of DeepONet based on concatenated task inputs and show that, from a worst-case approximation-complexity perspective, both architectures satisfy essentially the same asymptotic rates.

</details>


### [91] [Calibration, Uncertainty Communication, and Deployment Readiness in CKD Risk Prediction: A Framework Evaluation Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21566)
*Michael O. Eniolade*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CKD预测模型在内部数据上表现完美但存在过拟合，外部测试表明其校准和一致性预测均失效，证明仅凭内部AUROC不足以支持临床决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有慢性肾脏病（CKD）机器学习预测模型多关注判别性能（AUROC），严重忽视了校准和不确定性量化，导致临床医生无法判断模型输出概率的可靠性。

Method: 使用UCI CKD数据集训练五种分类器（逻辑回归、随机森林、XGBoost、支持向量机、高斯朴素贝叶斯）；采用期望校准误差（ECE）和Brier分数评估校准质量；应用拆分一致性预测（Split Conformal Prediction）以实现90%的有效覆盖率；最后在MIMIC-IV数据集上进行分布应力测试（评估患病率偏移和特征缺失情况），并结合八项部署就绪框架进行评估。

Result: 所有模型在UCI内部测试集上均达到1.00的AUROC。但在外部MIMIC-IV数据上，AUROC降至0.48-0.58，ECE激增，置信覆盖率显著低于目标值（90%降至21%-25%），且所有模型在临床部署就绪框架中评分均低于4分（满分16分）。

Conclusion: 仅在内部测试集上表现优异的模型往往无法泛化，且在临床部署前若缺乏外部验证（特别是针对校准稳定性和一致性预测覆盖率的验证），风险极高。

Abstract: Machine learning models for chronic kidney disease (CKD) risk prediction often post strong discrimination scores on internal test sets. Calibration and uncertainty quantification get far less attention, leaving clinicians without reliable information about whether the probability outputs are accurate.
  We trained five classifiers on the UCI CKD dataset (400 patients, 62.5% CKD prevalence): logistic regression, random forest, XGBoost, SVM with Platt scaling, and Gaussian naive Bayes. We evaluated each across calibration quality, conformal prediction coverage, and an eight-criterion deployment readiness framework. A distributional stress-test applied the best-calibrated variant of each model to the open-access MIMIC-IV demo cohort (97 patients, 23.7% CKD) to assess behaviour under prevalence shift and feature missingness. We measured calibration before and after Platt scaling and isotonic regression using Expected Calibration Error and Brier Score, and quantified uncertainty through split conformal prediction targeting 90% marginal coverage.
  All five models reached AUROC 1.00 on the UCI test set. Isotonic recalibration reduced internal ECE to 0.000-0.022. On MIMIC-IV, AUROC fell to 0.48-0.58, ECE rose to 0.68-0.76, and conformal coverage dropped from 0.80-0.98 to 0.21-0.25 against a 90% target. No model scored above 4 out of 16 on the deployment readiness checklist.
  Near-perfect internal performance did not transfer. Calibration stability and conformal coverage should be evaluated on external data before any clinical prediction model moves toward deployment.

</details>


### [92] [Plug-in Losses for Evidential Deep Learning: A Simplified Framework for Uncertainty Estimation that Includes the Softmax Classifier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22746)
*Berk Hayta,Hannah Laus,Simon Mittermaier,Felix Krahmer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对证据深度学习（EDL）目标函数实现复杂的问题，本文提出了一种基于均值的近似优化方法，在显著简化训练流程的同时，保持了与传统EDL相当的不确定性估计性能，并成功应用于语音识别任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的证据深度学习（EDL）方法虽然能实现单次前向传播的不确定性估计，但其目标函数高度复杂且难以实现，这限制了其在实时传感器学习系统中的应用。作者旨在简化EDL的训练目标，使其能更高效地部署，同时保持可靠的不确定性估计性能。

Method: 提出了一种基于狄利克雷均值的近似插件式（plug-in）损失函数。作者证明了在温和假设下，通过该近似方法产生的误差会随着证据的增加而衰减。该方法允许在标准深度学习框架中直接使用常见的损失函数（如MSE、交叉熵）进行训练，而无需复杂的狄利克雷期望目标。

Result: 在Google Speech Commands数据集上的实验表明，所提出的简化目标函数在预测精度和选择性预测能力上与经典EDL相当，但训练流程更简单。该研究首次在语音识别任务中实现了基于EDL的覆盖率-准确率权衡分析。

Conclusion: 研究证明，通过对狄利克雷期望目标的简化（插件式损失函数），可以在不牺牲预测准确性和不确定性量化性能的前提下，显著降低证据深度学习（EDL）的实现复杂度和计算难度。此简化方案为不确定性估计提供了一个更易于部署的框架，并从理论上论证了它与标准Softmax分类器的兼容性。

Abstract: Real-world sensor-based learning systems require uncertainty estimation that is both reliable and computationally efficient. Evidential Deep Learning (EDL) provides single-pass uncertainty estimation by modeling the class probabilities via Dirichlet distributions, where the Dirichlet parameters are predicted by a learned neural network mapping. However, this approach can lead to computational challenges, as Dirichlet expected objectives are more complex than standard supervised learning losses, complicating their analysis and implementation. We address this issue by approximating the objective of the first-order empirical risk minimization problem induced by EDL with a plug-in loss evaluated at the Dirichlet mean and show that, under mild assumptions, the approximation error decays with growing evidence for a broad class of loss functions, including mean-squared error and cross-entropy loss. As a special case, our analysis provides justification for the use of softmax in the context of uncertainty estimation, since under a particular evidence-to-Dirichlet mapping, our framework includes the standard softmax classifier. We validate the proposed simplified objectives on the Google Speech Commands dataset and show that they achieve predictive accuracy and selective prediction performance comparable to classical EDL, while being simpler to implement using standard deep learning losses and training pipelines. To the best of our knowledge, this empirical analysis is the first to obtain coverage-accuracy trade-offs for speech recognition tasks through EDL.

</details>


### [93] [ConTact: Contact-First Antibody CDR Design via Explicit Interface Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21600)
*Mansoor Ahmed,Spencer VonBank,Nadeem Taj,Sujin Lee,Naila Jan,Murray Patterson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ConTact 架构通过将抗体 CDR 设计解耦为接触点预测和序列生成两个阶段，显著提升了抗体设计的结构质量与表位匹配能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的计算抗体 CDR 设计模型将“确定接触位置”与“选择氨基酸”这两个截然不同的子问题混为一谈，导致模型在处理过程中对抗原信号的利用不够聚焦，难以有效执行接触推理。

Method: 提出了一种名为“接触后优化”（contact-then-act）的架构，包含三个级联阶段：学习表面互补性指纹、预测 CDR-抗原接触点，以及通过接触门控机制注入抗原特征。此外，引入了距离偏置的交叉注意力模块和接触加权交叉熵损失函数。

Result: 在 CHIMERA-Bench 数据集上表现最优，RMSD 提升 7%，表位感知 F1 分数提升 10%，序列恢复率 (AAR) 达到 0.38。

Conclusion: ConTact 通过明确的分阶段建模（触点识别 + 氨基酸序列设计）成功解决了抗体 CDR 设计中“接触推理”与“序列生成”任务耦合的问题，在提升结构稳健性和表位感知能力方面效果显著。

Abstract: Computational antibody CDR design methods condition on antigen structure to generate binding loops, yet existing architectures conflate two fundamentally distinct sub-problems: identifying which CDR positions will contact the antigen, and selecting amino acids at those positions. This conflation forces models to learn contact reasoning implicitly through uniform message passing, diluting antigen signal across all positions equally. We introduce ConTact, a contact-then-act architecture that explicitly decomposes CDR design into three cascaded stages: learning surface complementarity fingerprints, predicting CDR-antigen contacts, and injecting contact-gated antigen features into the sequence head. A distance-biased cross-attention module encodes geometric priors favoring spatial neighbors, while a contact-weighted cross-entropy loss concentrates gradient signal on binding-critical positions. On CHIMERA-Bench dataset, ConTact achieves the best structural quality (7% RMSD improvement over the next-best baseline), best epitope awareness (10% F1 score over GNN baselines), and competitive sequence recovery (AAR 0.38) among several CDR-H3 design baselines.

</details>


### [94] [Uniform Diffusion Models Revisited: Leave-One-Out Denoiser and Absorbing State Reformulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22765)
*Samson Gourevitch,Yazid Janati,Dario Shariatian,Umut Simsekli,Eric Moulines,Eric P. Xing,Alain Durmus*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文分析了离散扩散模型中训练目标与参数化之间的失配问题，提出使用“留一法”参数化来优化UDM，并引入吸收态重构，证明了采样设计和参数化选择对性能的影响优于边缘分布的选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散扩散模型（如MDM和UDM）在训练方式（预测干净数据）与反向动力学定义之间存在不一致，传统UDM的插件式ELBO优化目标与常见的交叉熵去噪目标存在失配，导致两者性能差异。

Method: 1. 分析了UDM中标准插件桥参数化与去噪后验之间的不匹配问题，指出其实际优化目标是“留一法后验”。2. 推导了去噪器、留一法后验与得分函数之间的精确转换关系，以解耦参数化与训练目标。3. 引入了“吸收态重构”（absorbing-state reformulation）的均匀扩散模型。4. 设计了改进的预测器-校正器采样器及基于留一法预测的温度采样策略。

Result: 在语言模型任务中，留一法参数化一致地提升了UDM的生成性能，而新的吸收态重构方法在性能上匹配或超越了MDM。改进的采样策略无需额外训练即可提升生成质量。

Conclusion: 离散扩散模型的性能差异在很大程度上并非源于边缘分布的选择，而是源于参数化和采样设计。通过采用留一法（leave-one-out）预测及引入吸收态重构，可以显著提升均匀扩散模型（UDM）的生成质量并缩小其与掩码扩散模型（MDM）的差距。

Abstract: Discrete diffusion models are often trained through clean-data prediction, but the prediction can be used in different ways to define the reverse dynamics. In Masked Diffusion Models (MDM) these choices largely coincide, whereas in Uniform Diffusion Models (UDM) they do not. We show that the standard plug-in bridge parameterization for UDM is not optimized by the denoising posterior, but by a leave-one-out posterior that predicts each clean token without using its own noisy observation. This identifies a mismatch between the plug-in ELBO and the usual cross-entropy denoising objective. We characterize the leave-one-out target and derive exact conversions between the denoiser, the leave-one-out posterior, and the score. These conversions allow us to disentangle parameterization and training objective. Our results also lead to inference improvements without any additional training through an informed predictor-corrector sampler and improved temperature sampling based on the leave-one-out predictor.
  We further introduce an absorbing-state reformulation of uniform diffusion that preserves the UDM joint law while decomposing it into masked-diffusion-like sampling operations, with simpler denoising posteriors, carry-over unmasking, and a natural remasking mechanism. On language modeling, leave-one-out parameterizations consistently improve UDM generation, while the absorbing construction matches or surpasses masked diffusion. These results suggest that the empirical gap between masked and uniform diffusion is driven less by the choice of marginals themselves than by parameterization and sampling design. The code and models can be found at https://github.com/samsongourevitch/rev_udm.

</details>


### [95] [When Are Teacher Tokens Reliable? Position-Weighted On-Policy Self-Distillation for Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21606)
*Xiaogeng Liu,Xinyan Wang,Yingzi Ma,Yechao Zhang,Chaowei Xiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现推理任务中教师模型的Token可靠性具有位置相关性，据此提出的PW-OPSD通过位置加权优化了自蒸馏过程，在无需额外计算开销的情况下提升了模型推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自蒸馏方法对所有生成的Token赋予相同的权重，忽略了教师模型在推理不同步骤中的可靠性存在差异；而传统的基于熵（Entropy）的方法无法区分“不确定性”与“解的多样性”，导致对教师反馈利用不当。

Method: 1. 提出了“分支可行性诊断”（branch-viability diagnostic）来评估教师模型在推理过程中各个位置的可靠性；2. 发现序列中位置越靠后的Token，其重要性通常高于靠前的Token；3. 提出了位置加权在线策略自蒸馏（PW-OPSD）算法，通过对序列位置进行加权来指导学生模型的训练。

Result: 在Qwen3-4B模型上，位置得分预测教师可靠性的AUROC达到0.83（远高于局部不确定性得分的0.57）；PW-OPSD在AIME 2024和AIME 2025测试集上分别提升了1.0和1.1个点，并在多个更大规模模型中展现了良好的泛化性。

Conclusion: 推理模型的知识蒸馏中，教师模型生成的Token可靠性与位置（Position）具有强相关性；通过引入位置加权机制（PW-OPSD），可以在无需增加额外计算成本的前提下，显著提升模型在推理任务（如AIME）上的表现。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) trains a student on its own rollouts using a privileged teacher, but its standard objective weights all generated tokens equally, implicitly treating the privileged teacher target as equally reliable at every student-visited prefix. Existing entropy-based OPD methods relax this uniformity by modulating token-level supervision with teacher entropy, but high teacher entropy in reasoning has an ambiguous reliability meaning: it can reflect either non-viable uncertainty or benign solution diversity. To identify this phenomenon, we introduce a branch-viability diagnostic. Specifically, we record next-token alternatives from the privileged-answer teacher prompt, force each alternative after the student prompt plus its on-policy spine prefix, and test whether the resulting student-template continuation recovers the correct answer. On Qwen3-4B, we find that an oriented within-sequence position score is the strongest tested predictor of teacher-token reliability, reaching an area-under-ROC-curve (AUROC) of 0.83; local uncertainty scores are at most 0.57. Motivated by this trajectory-level structure, we propose Position-Weighted On-Policy Self-Distillation (PW-OPSD), which applies an increasing position weight while keeping the same student rollout, privileged teacher pass, and clipped forward-KL target as OPSD. In our comprehensive evaluations with different random seeds, the diagnostic-derived PW-OPSD improves AIME 2024 and AIME 2025 Avg@12 by +1.0 and +1.1 points, and a generalization evaluation on two larger-scale models from different families, DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B and Olmo-3-7B-Think, also demonstrates consistent aggregate Avg@12 improvements. These results show that teacher-token reliability in reasoning distillation is trajectory-structured and can be utilized without additional teacher computation.

</details>


### [96] [SDPM: Survival Diffusion Probabilistic Model for Continuous-Time Survival Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22776)
*Stanislav R. Kirpichenko,Andrei V. Konstantinov,Lev V. Utkin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SDPM 是一种新型的生成式生存分析模型，通过扩散模型直接对连续生存分布建模，无需假设风险函数形式，在预测准确度和分布还原能力上均表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生存分析方法往往依赖于对风险函数的结构假设，或者需要对时间轴进行离散化，这不仅限制了模型的灵活性，还引入了近似误差，难以准确捕捉复杂的连续时间事件分布。

Method: 提出生存扩散概率模型（SDPM），通过去噪扩散模型对生存结果（观察时间 T 和删失指标 δ）的条件概率进行建模；采用标准化对数时间变换及高斯混合表示，将生存时间预测转化为生成任务；利用 Kaplan-Meier 估计器从生成的样本中推导生存函数，避免了对风险函数的假设和时间离散化。

Result: 在十个真实数据集上的测试表明，SDPM 在 C-index、时序 AUC 和 Brier 分数等指标上均优于或媲美现有的树模型、梯度提升及深度生存模型；在合成数据实验中展现了更强的分布重构能力。

Conclusion: SDPM 提出了一种基于扩散概率模型的生存分析新框架。该方法有效地克服了传统参数化模型假设限制和离散化误差的问题，同时通过数据驱动的生成方式实现了对连续生存分布的精准重构，在多个指标上表现出强劲的竞争力。

Abstract: Survival analysis aims to estimate a time-to-event distribution from data with censored observations. Many existing methods either impose structural assumptions on the hazard function or discretize the time axis, which may limit flexibility and introduce approximation errors. We propose the Survival Diffusion Probabilistic Model (SDPM), a generative approach to continuous-time survival analysis. SDPM models the conditional distribution of the survival outcome, represented by the pair of observed time and censoring indicator, $\mathbb{P}(T,δ\mid \mathbf{x})$, using a denoising diffusion model. Under the assumption of conditionally independent censoring, conditional samples generated by the model can be transformed into survival function estimates using the Kaplan-Meier estimator. This formulation avoids parametric assumptions on the event-time distribution and does not require a discretization of the output time space. The model operates in a transformed target space, using standardized log-times and a continuous Gaussian-mixture representation of the censoring indicator. We evaluate SDPM on ten real survival datasets and compare it with five strong baselines, including tree-based, boosting-based, and neural survival models. Results show that SDPM achieves competitive predictive performance across C-index, integrated time-dependent AUC, and integrated Brier score. A study on synthetic Cox-Weibull data demonstrates that SDPM can recover the shape of an underlying continuous survival distribution more accurately than a strong nonparametric baseline when sufficiently many samples are generated. An ablation study confirms the importance of the proposed target-space transformations, which improve event-rate calibration, reduce invalid generated times, and provide consistent gains in predictive discrimination. Codes implementing the proposed model are publicly available.

</details>


### [97] [The Matching Principle: A Geometric Theory of Loss Functions for Nuisance-Robust Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22800)
*Vishal Rajput*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种统一的鲁棒性理论，认为目前的各类鲁棒性技术本质上都是在针对“部署干扰的协方差”进行正则化。作者通过理论证明和大规模实证，验证了基于干扰协方差的雅可比矩阵正则化能够系统性地提升模型性能和诚实度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的鲁棒性研究（如领域自适应、遮挡不变性、时间鲁棒性等）通常被视为独立问题并采用不同的方法。作者旨在指出这些方法共享一个深层的统计结构，并试图将其统一到一个理论框架下。

Method: 1. 提出了“匹配原则（matching principle）”：通过估计扰动协方差来加权雅可比正则化。
2. 在线性高斯模型下证明了闭式最优性及正则化范围的必要性。
3. 引入了“轨迹偏差指数（TDI）”，作为一种无标签的嵌入敏感度探测工具。
4. 对从传统机器学习到 Qwen2.5-7B 的十三组模型进行了实证验证（Falsification tests）。

Result: 1. 证明了在匹配空间内进行“立方根注水（cube-root water-filling）”是闭式最优的。
2. 实验验证了预测的“匹配 > 各向同性 > 错误正则化”的性能排序规律。
3. 在大模型（7B）规模下，验证了该方法在提升选择性诚实度（selective honesty）的同时，比 DPO 更能保留风格轨迹一致性。

Conclusion: 文章提出了一套统一的鲁棒性理论框架，即通过估计“标签保持部署干扰（label-preserving deployment nuisance）”的协方差，并据此对编码器雅可比矩阵（encoder Jacobian）进行正则化。该方法论证了许多看似独立的鲁棒性技术（如对抗训练、IRM、数据增强等）本质上都是在估计同一个统计对象，而非独立的技巧。

Abstract: Robustness, domain adaptation, photometric and occlusion invariance, compositional generalisation, temporal robustness, alignment safety, and classical anisotropic regularisation are usually treated as separate problems with separate method families. This paper argues that much of their shared structure is one statistical problem: estimate the covariance of label-preserving deployment nuisance, then regularise the encoder Jacobian along a matrix whose range covers that covariance (the matching principle). CORAL, adversarial training, IRM, augmentation, metric learning, Jacobian penalties, and alignment-style constraints are different estimators of that object, not independent robustness tricks.
  In the linear-Gaussian model we prove closed-form optimality (Theorem A), including cube-root water-filling within the matched range; necessity of range coverage for quadratic Jacobian penalties (Theorem G); the same range dichotomy at deep global minima; and two falsification controls (Lemma C; Corollaries E), with seven conditional consistency lemmas (D1-D7) for estimation under standard identifiability assumptions.
  We introduce the Trajectory Deviation Index (TDI), a label-free probe of embedding sensitivity when task accuracy or Jacobian Frobenius norm is insufficient.
  Thirteen pre-registered blocks from classical ML through Qwen2.5-7B test the predicted matched, then isotropic, then wrong-W ordering on geometry and deployment drift; twelve pass, and the sole exception (Office-31) is an eigengap failure named before the run. At 7B scale, matched style-PMH improves selective honesty and preserves Style TDI where standard DPO degrades it.
  The contribution is naming the deployment nuisance covariance, stating what the regulariser must do, and supplying a closed-form falsifiable theory once that object is identified, not universality on every leaderboard.

</details>


### [98] [$\textit{BlockFormer}$ : Transformer-based inference from interaction maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21617)
*Eloïse Touron,Pedro L. C. Rodrigues,Julyan Arbel,Nelle Varoquaux,Michael Arbel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本论文提出了一种利用Transformer架构和合成数据训练的通用方法，能从Hi-C相互作用图中自动且高精度地识别出着丝粒的位置，有效应对了基因组数据特征的多变性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从Hi-C等基因组相互作用图中识别特定基因组特征（如着丝粒）是一个典型的逆问题，现有的分析方法难以同时处理数据中实体数量和尺寸的高度可变性。

Method: 采用基于Transformer的架构来处理输入数据中实体数量和尺寸的可变性；结合自定义模拟器生成大规模合成数据进行模型训练。

Result: 该方法成功在多种物种中实现了高精度的着丝粒定位，且在不同基因组尺度上表现稳健。

Conclusion: 提出了一种基于Transformer的通用数据驱动框架，能够有效解决从生物相互作用图（如Hi-C数据）中推断特定位置（如着丝粒）的逆问题，具有良好的跨物种泛化能力。

Abstract: Inference from interaction maps, such as centromere identification from genome-wide chromosome conformation capture techniques -- notably Hi-C -- can be formulated as a generic inverse problem: infer a set of parameters given a map summarizing pairwise interactions between entities through blocks of variable numbers and sizes. In this work, we introduce a data-driven approach that leverages shared structure between these maps, such as global alignment between localized patterns, while handling the variability in number and size of entities arising in real-world data. Our approach relies on a transformer architecture capable of handling such variability and a custom simulator to generate abundant, yet computationally cheap synthetic data for training. Applied to the problem of centromere localization, the method accurately recovers their genomic positions across a wide range of species of various genome sizes.

</details>


### [99] [EntmaxKV: Support-Aware Decoding for Entmax Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21649)
*Gonçalo Duarte,Miguel Couceiro,Marcos V. Treviso*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EntmaxKV 是一种利用 α-entmax 稀疏性进行长上下文解码的框架，通过在加载 KV 页面前识别“支持集”，在保证计算精确性的前提下，显著降低了内存流量并提升了长上下文推理速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文解码受到 KV 缓存内存带宽的限制。现有的基于 softmax 的稀疏解码方法属于“稠密尾部近似”，会导致信息丢失；而 α-entmax 具有产生精确零点的特性，理论上支持“支持集恢复（support recovery）”，即若选中的候选项包含 entmax 支持集，解码结果可保持完全精确。

Method: 引入 EntmaxKV 框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 查询感知页面评分（Query-aware page scoring）；(2) 支持感知候选选择（Support-aware candidate selection）；(3) 稀疏 entmax 注意力。并使用 Gaussian-aware entmax 选择器从轻量级页面统计量中估计 entmax 阈值，以动态调整计算预算。

Result: EntmaxKV 在匹配的 KV 缓存预算下，比 softmax 稀疏化方法丢弃更少的概率质量，保留了更多支持集 Token；在 1M 上下文长度测试中，相比完整注意力基线，实现了高达 3.36 倍（相较 softmax 稀疏化）至 5.43 倍（相较 entmax 密集推理）的加速。

Conclusion: EntmaxKV 证明了利用 α-entmax 的稀疏特性，可以实现精确的稀疏解码，在大幅降低 KV 缓存内存带宽需求的同时，保持与全缓存注意力机制相当的模型性能。该方法在 1M 上下文长度测试中实现了显著的推理加速。

Abstract: Long-context decoding is increasingly limited by KV-cache memory traffic since each generated token attends over a cache whose size grows linearly with context length. Existing sparse decoding methods reduce this cost by selecting subsets of tokens or pages, but are designed for softmax attention, whose dense tails make any truncation discard nonzero probability mass. In contrast, $α$-entmax produces exact zeros, turning sparse decoding from dense-tail approximation into support recovery: if the selected candidates contain the entmax support, sparse decoding remains exact. While recent entmax kernels enable efficient training, they do not address the autoregressive decoding bottleneck, where dense inference still streams the full KV cache before sparsity is known. In this work, we introduce EntmaxKV, an entmax-native sparse decoding framework that exploits sparsity before KV pages are loaded. EntmaxKV combines query-aware page scoring, support-aware candidate selection, and sparse entmax attention. We analyze truncation error through the dropped probability mass $δ$, showing that output error is controlled by $δ$ and vanishes when the entmax support is recovered. We further introduce a Gaussian-aware entmax selector that estimates the entmax threshold from lightweight page statistics, adapting the selected budget to the score distribution. Empirically, EntmaxKV drops less probability mass, retains more support tokens, and achieves lower output error than softmax-based sparse decoding at matched KV budgets. On long-context and language modeling benchmarks, it closely matches full-cache entmax while using a small fraction of the KV cache, achieving up to $3.36\times$ (softmax) and $5.43\times$ (entmax) speedup over full attention baselines at 1M context length. Code available at: https://github.com/deep-spin/entmaxkv.

</details>


### [100] [Amplifying, Not Learning: Fine-Tuned AI Text Detectors Amplify a Pretrained Direction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21653)
*Alexander Smirnov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AI文本检测器并非真的会区分人类与AI，而是利用了模型预训练中固有的“典型性”特征。研究通过实验证实，简单的几何投影往往优于复杂的微调，且现有检测器的提升主要归功于校准偏置的调整。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究AI文本检测器的底层工作原理，检验检测器是否真正学会了区分AI与人类写作，还是仅仅在依赖预训练模型中固有的分布特征（典型性）。

Method: 通过几何投影、雅可比预测器（Jacobian predictor）以及三种操作上不同的探针（探测caps_rate残差化、几何epsilon消融、闭式文本对预测器）对预训练编码器进行分析。对比了Raw编码器投影与微调（Fine-tuning）方案的效能，并利用ESL（非母语）写作数据验证了“典型性轴”翻转的预测。

Result: 未经微调的Raw编码器投影即可达到接近甚至超越全量微调的检测性能；在非母语写作中观察到了典型性轴的翻转；提出的闭式雅可比预测器大幅提升了检测器的查全率（TPR），并能有效泛化至第三方检测器；证实了现有检测方案的性能增益主要源于校准偏移而非表征学习。

Conclusion: AI文本检测器的核心机制并非建立“AI对人”的判别边界，而是放大预训练模型中固有的“典型性（typicality）”轴。现有的检测方法主要是通过校准偏移（calibration shift）来实现，而非学习到了新的判别性表征。

Abstract: AI text detectors amplify a pretrained typicality axis; they do not construct an AI-vs-human boundary. On raw encoders before any task supervision, projecting onto centroid(AI)-centroid(HC3) achieves NYT-vs-HC3 AUROC 0.806/0.944/0.834 across three architectures (86-106% of the fine-tuned discrimination ceiling: on RoBERTa-base, raw projection exceeds fine-tuning); on RoBERTa-base, full fine-tuning reduces discrimination below raw on both fluent-formal populations tested. The same axis inverts on non-native ESL writing (AUROC 0.06-0.20) -- a falsifiable prediction unique to the typicality reading. A 24-example frozen probe matches full fine-tuning (0.900 vs 0.895). A closed-form Jacobian predictor parameterises axis-manipulating interventions with R^2 = 1.000 universal, lifts ELECTRA-CE deployment TPR from 0.000 to 0.904 at FPR = 1%, and transfers to three independently-trained third-party RoBERTa detectors at 16/16 oracle-equivalence (57% NYT-FPR reduction on the OpenAI detector). Scope: encoder family; mechanism magnitude HC3-anchored; population-level shared axis with per-text mechanisms varying across architectures. Three operationally distinct probes -- text-surface caps_rate residualisation, geometric signed-epsilon ablation, closed-form text-pair predictor -- agree at cos 0.74/0.81/1.00 across three architectures, confirming observer-invariance. Under matched-TPR-0.90 evaluation, the published intervention zoo (CC, dealign-f2c) is calibration-equivalent across 27 cells (|Delta AUROC| <= 0.0081), and >= 97% of the LoRA->full-FT bias gap on ELECTRA is calibration shift, not learned representation -- the central claim's prediction confirmed.

</details>


### [101] [Value-Gradient Hypothesis of RL for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21654)
*Arip Asadulaev,Daniil Ognev,Karim Salta,Martin Takac*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从价值梯度视角解释了无评论员强化学习在LLM后训练中的工作机制，并确定了强化学习在预训练过程中产生最大收益的理论条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管PPO和GRPO等无评论员强化学习方法在大语言模型（LLM）微调中表现出色，但其背后的理论机制（为什么有效）以及其最优应用场景尚不明确。

Method: 1. 基于可微向前传播（differentiable rollout）和加性噪声参数化，推导了无评论员（critic-free）策略更新与价值梯度的数学等价性。
2. 针对离散Transformer策略，分析了通过注意力机制的反向传播（autodifferentiation）如何产生近似价值信号的经验协同状态（empirical costates）。

Result: 证明了无评论员方法在期望下进行价值梯度更新，并量化了通过自动微分产生的价值近似误差（受采样间隙和策略熵控制）。

Conclusion: 研究提出了强化学习收益的分解框架（价值梯度信号与可达奖励空间），并推导出强化学习在预训练轨迹中发挥最大效能的明确准则。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning substantially improves pretrained language models, but it remains understudied why critic-free methods such as PPO and GRPO work as well as they do, and when they should provide the largest gains. We develop a value-gradient perspective of critic-free RL for LLM post-training. First, under a differentiable rollout and additive-noise parameterization, we show that the actor update is value-gradient-like in expectation: the backward pass propagates costates whose conditional expectation equals the value gradient. Second, for discrete transformer policies, we show that autodifferentiation through attention produces empirical costates that approximate this value signal, with an error controlled by the sampling gap and policy entropy. These results motivate a decomposition of RL impact into value gradient signal and reachable reward headroom, yielding a criterion for when RL should be most effective along a pretraining trajectory.

</details>


### [102] [I-SAFE: Wasserstein Coherence Metrics for Structural Auditing of Scientific AI Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21731)
*Barbara Tarantino,Gennaro Auricchio,Paolo Giudici*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: I-SAFE 是一个模型无关的审计框架，通过利用领域先验对输入进行扰动，评估科学 AI 模型是否真正遵循领域结构逻辑，而非仅仅依赖数据相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学领域的深度学习模型常通过快捷特征或偏差实现高精度，而非学习到科学逻辑。传统性能指标难以评估模型对领域结构先验的遵循程度，存在“盲目信任”的风险。

Method: 提出 I-SAFE 框架，核心是基于 Wasserstein 一致性度量（WCM）的多维度审计方法，包括基于分位数的度量（QBM）、原始 WCM 以及平移不变 WCM，旨在通过结构化扰动观察模型输出的分布协同性。

Result: 将 I-SAFE 应用于 DTI 预测（DeepConvDTI、DeepDTA、TAPB），发现在准确率相当的情况下，模型在结构扰动下的分布响应剖面存在显著差异，揭示了仅靠准确率评估的局限性。

Conclusion: I-SAFE 框架通过引入外部领域结构先验，揭示了传统深度学习模型评分之外的分布特性差异，为诊断模型科学合理性提供了有效的审计工具。

Abstract: Deep learning models are increasingly used in scientific prediction tasks where strong benchmark performance is often interpreted as evidence of scientifically meaningful behavior. This interpretation is fragile, as models may exploit shortcut features, dataset-specific regularities, or distributional biases that are predictive on held-out data but not aligned with domain-relevant structure. To address this limitation, we introduce the \textsc{I-SAFE} (Interventional Secure, Accurate, Fair and Explainable) framework, a post-hoc distributional auditing framework for scientific AI models centered on the Wasserstein Coherence Metric (WCM). Given a trained black-box predictor and an external structural prior encoding domain knowledge about task-relevant input structure, \textsc{I-SAFE} evaluates raw model outputs under structurally guided perturbations of the input. The proposed audit measures output-distribution coherence through three complementary metrics: a Quantile-Based Metric (QBM) for location-level coherence, the WCM for ordinal coherence, and a translation-invariant WCM variant for shape coherence. We instantiate \textsc{I-SAFE} on drug--target interaction (DTI) prediction using the Davis kinase benchmark, KLIFS (Kinase--Ligand Interaction Fingerprints and Structures) binding-pocket annotations, and three sequence-based DTI models: DeepConvDTI, DeepDTA, and TAPB. Although the models operate in a comparable predictive regime, \textsc{I-SAFE} reveals substantially different distributional response profiles, a distinction invisible to accuracy-based evaluation. The framework is model-agnostic and applicable to any domain where inputs admit a structured decomposition and an external prior is available.

</details>


### [103] [stable-worldmodel: A Platform for Reproducible World Modeling Research and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21800)
*Lucas Maes,Quentin Le Lidec,Luiz Facury,Nassim Massaudi,Ayush Chaurasia,Francesco Capuano,Richard Gao,Taj Gillin,Dan Haramati,Damien Scieur,Yann LeCun,Randall Balestriero*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 为缓解世界模型研究中的碎片化与效率问题，swm 平台提供了一个高性能、标准化、且具备系统化评估能力的开源框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前世界模型研究领域存在三大瓶颈：代码基库碎片化（缺乏复用性）、视频数据加载性能低下、以及缺乏系统化的泛化能力评估标准。

Method: 构建了一个名为 stable-worldmodel (swm) 的开源平台，包含：(1) 基于 LanceDB 的高性能数据层；(2) 模块化、标准化的世界模型基线算法与规划求解器实现；(3) 具备可控变量的综合环境评估套件。

Result: 该平台实现了多数据源兼容（MP4/HDF5/LeRobot）、集成了前沿基线算法，并提供了视觉、几何与物理等多维度的系统化评估工具。

Conclusion: swm 通过提供统一、可扩展的开发框架，解决了世界模型研究中碎片化、缺乏标准及效率低下的问题，旨在显著降低科研门槛，加速实现可靠的世界模型。

Abstract: World models are central to building agents that can reason, plan, and generalize beyond their training data. However, research on world models is currently fragmented, with disparate codebases, data pipelines, and evaluation protocols hindering reproducibility and fair comparison. Current practice is further limited by three key bottlenecks: fragile one-off codebases, slow video data loading, and the lack of standardized generalization benchmarks. We present stable-worldmodel (swm), an open-source platform for standardized and reproducible world modeling research and evaluation. It delivers (1) a high-performance Lance-based data layer with native support and conversion tools for MP4, HDF5, and LeRobot datasets, (2) clean, well-tested implementations of modern world model baselines and planning solvers, and (3) a broad suite of environments and tasks extended with controllable visual, geometric, and physical factors of variation for systematic in-silico evaluation of dynamics understanding, control performance, representation quality, and out-of-distribution generalization. By unifying the full pipeline under a single, scalable framework, \texttt{swm} dramatically reduces research overhead and accelerates trustworthy progress toward reliable world models.

</details>


### [104] [Quantitative coronary calcification analysis for prediction of myocardial ischemia using non-contrast CT calcium scoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21745)
*Juhwan Lee,Sadeer Al-Kindi,Ammar Hoori,Tao Hu,Hao Wu,Justin N. Kim,Robert Gilkeson,Sanjay Rajagopalan,David L. Wilson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究通过引入“钙化组学”特征，训练出的机器学习模型能从常规非增强CT扫描中有效预测心肌缺血，其表现优于传统临床评分方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在开发一种新型机器学习框架，利用常规非增强CTCS（计算机断层扫描钙化评分）定量评估数据来预测心肌缺血，从而提高心血管风险评估的有效性和可及性。

Method: 利用1,375名患者的数据，提取临床变量、Agatston评分及钙化组学（calcium-omics）特征共74个变量；使用XGBoost模型结合SHAP进行特征筛选，并通过5折交叉验证评估模型性能。

Result: 模型实现了98.9%的精确率、79.2%的敏感度和87.7%的F1分数；钙化组学特征的加入显著提升了预测性能（p<0.05）；逻辑回归显示钙化动脉数量与心肌缺血关联最强（OR: 3.63）。

Conclusion: 该研究开发了一种基于常规非增强CTCS扫描的机器学习模型，通过引入钙化组学特征，实现了比仅使用临床变量和Agatston评分更高的心肌缺血预测准确性，提供了一种更 accessibles 的心血管风险分层方法。

Abstract: Non-contrast computed tomography calcium scoring (CTCS) is widely recognized as an effective tool for cardiovascular risk stratification. This study aimed to develop a novel machine learning framework for predicting myocardial ischemia from routine non-contrast CTCS scans using quantitative coronary calcium assessment. This study analyzed 1,375 patients who underwent both non-contrast CTCS and regadenoson stress cardiac positron emission tomography myocardial perfusion imaging within one year at University Hospitals Cleveland Medical Center. A total of 74 variables, including clinical variables, Agatston score, and calcium-omics features, were evaluated. Relevant features were identified using XGBoost with Shapley Additive exPlanations (SHAP). Predictive models were trained and evaluated using 5-fold cross-validation. Among 987 patients, 89 (9%) were positive for myocardial ischemia. The final model incorporated the Agatston score, eight calcium-omics features, and age. The proposed model achieved a precision of 98.9+/-3.0%, sensitivity of 79.2+/-8.4, and F1 score of 87.7+/-5.3%. The addition of calcium-omics features significantly improved predictive performance compared with models using clinical variables alone or clinical variables with the Agatston score (p<0.05). Interestingly, the number of calcified arteries, despite being the lowest-ranked feature based on SHAP analysis, showed the strongest association with myocardial ischemia in logistic regression analysis (odds ratio: 3.63, 95% confidence interval: 2.80-4.77, p<0.00001). We developed a machine learning approach for predicting myocardial ischemia using routinely acquired non-contrast CTCS scans. Calcium-omics features provided incremental predictive value beyond conventional risk factors and Agatston scoring and may support more accessible cardiovascular risk stratification.

</details>


### [105] [Beyond Euclidean Proximity: Repairing Latent World Models with Horizon-Matched Trajectory Reachability Metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22164)
*Liangyu Li,Shengzhi Wang,Qingwen Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TRM 是一种针对预训练潜在世界模型的终端排序修复方法。通过视界感知的成对学习替换原始的潜在欧氏距离度量，显著缓解了 MPC 在复杂环境中因距离度量失效导致的规划失败问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的潜在模型预测控制（MPC）通常使用潜在空间中的欧氏距离来衡量终端状态与目标状态之间的代价，但这假设了潜在空间中的距离能准确反映可达性，而在复杂场景下这一假设往往不成立，导致规划器做出错误决策。

Method: 提出轨迹可达性度量（TRM）方法，在固定好的潜在世界模型基础上，通过利用轨迹数据训练一个小的成对头部（pairwise head），作为替代或混合的终端代价函数。关键设计是“视界感知监督”（horizon-aware supervision），即在广泛且平衡的时间跨度上进行训练以匹配长视界规划需求。

Result: 在 TwoRoom 基准测试中，TRM 将 LeWorldModel 的成功率从 7.0% 提升至 97.0%，将 PLDM 基准的成功率从 32.7% 提升至 84.0%。分析表明，TRM 纠正了潜在空间中 MSE 对关键位置信息的忽视，优化了候选序列的排序效果。

Conclusion: TRM 证明了在潜在空间中，简单的欧氏距离并不足以衡量终端状态的可达性。通过在预训练的潜在世界模型之上增加一个“视界感知”的成对判别训练，TRM 可以有效修复终端代价函数，显著提升模型预测控制（MPC）的成功率，且无需微调模型架构。

Abstract: Latent world models can contain the state needed for control, yet their terminal-cost interface can expose the planner to the wrong decision-relevant information. In common latent MPC, candidate sequences are ranked by Euclidean distance between predicted terminal and goal latent states; this assumes that raw latent distance weights reachability-relevant variables correctly. We propose trajectory reachability metrics (TRM), a post-hoc terminal-ranking method for fixed latent world models. TRM trains a small pairwise head from logged trajectory structure and uses it as a replacement or hybrid cost; the encoder, dynamics, sampler, optimizer, and evaluation manifests remain fixed. The key design choice is horizon-aware supervision: the metric is trained on broad, balanced temporal separations to match the long-horizon terminal candidate ranking problem. On a hard TwoRoom benchmark, raw latent planning with LeWorldModel (LeWM) reaches 7.0% success, while full-horizon TRM reaches 97.0%; shuffled temporal-label controls stay at 0.0%. The same recipe improves a PLDM baseline from 32.7% to 84.0% across three seeds, and a short-horizon TRM variant reaches only 35.0% with the 100,000 pair budget. In TwoRoom, we provide mechanistic evidence for why TRM works: XY position is linearly decodable (R^2=0.998), yet raw latent MSE misranks candidates; the XY-probe rowspace accounts for less than 1% of terminal-goal latent MSE but carries most candidate-quality signal; and SCSA audits show that TRM improves the ordering and selected endpoint seen by the planner. On PushT go50/go75, TRM-style task-state metrics improve SCSA ranking and selected final distance more cleanly than closed-loop success, motivating auxiliary hybrid costs in continuous manipulation. TRM is the planner-facing repair, and audits explain when terminal reachability metrics should replace or augment raw latent proximity.

</details>


### [106] [Models Can Model, But Can't Bind: Structured Grounding in Text-to-Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21751)
*Zhiqi Gao,Albert Ge,Alexander Berenbeim,Nathaniel D. Bastian,Frederic Sala*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现大模型在文本转优化任务中存在参数绑定瓶颈，作者提出了Text2Opt-Bench基准与BIND数据处理方法，通过将数据外部化与专门的绑定能力微调，显著提升了模型在复杂优化问题中的求解准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本转优化任务包含“建模”（选择架构）和“绑定”（参数映射）两部分。研究发现，随着问题数据规模（参数量）增加，现有大语言模型在将数据映射到优化变量时的准确率会大幅下降。

Method: 1. 构建了Text2Opt-Bench基准测试集；2. 提出了BIND（Binding Inference via Numeric Data-externalization）方法：将数值数据从Prompt中剥离，存入结构化文件，由模型编程方式调用；3. 进行了专门针对“绑定”能力的模型微调实验。

Result: 1. 模型在处理大规模实例数据时准确率大幅下降；2. BIND方法显著提升了模型性能（GPT-5-Nano从59.1%提升至82.4%，GPT-5从86.2%提升至95.8%）；3. 专门的Binding微调模型性能优于端到端SFT和RL，1.5B模型即可对标7B基线模型。

Conclusion: 大语言模型在文本转优化（Text-to-optimization）任务中存在“有效绑定极限”（effective binding limit），即随实例数据规模增大，绑定正确参数的能力会显著下降。通过将数值数据外部化至结构化文件并采用BIND机制，可显著提升模型性能，且证明了专门针对“绑定”能力进行训练比单纯的端到端微调（SFT）或强化学习（RL）更有效。

Abstract: Text-to-optimization requires two separable capabilities: modeling -- choosing the right optimization structure -- and binding -- grounding every coefficient, index, and parameter in the concrete problem data. We study this via Text2Opt-Bench, a scalable benchmark of solver-verified optimization problems spanning 12 categories, from textbook linear programs to stochastic and multi-objective formulations with up to thousands of variables. Across 10+ models, we find that accuracy collapses as instance data grows, even when the formulation itself is simple. We call this the effective binding limit. We address this via a simple inference-time approach, BIND, which externalizes numeric data to structured files so the model binds data programmatically rather than transcribing from the prompt. BIND improves GPT-5-Nano from 59.1% to 82.4% accuracy, matching pass@5 (82.0%) at lower token cost than pass@1, and GPT-5 from 86.2% to 95.8%. Furthermore, we validate our hypothesis by finetuning a model exclusively on binding and show that it outperforms end-to-end SFT and RL across three structurally distinct optimization categories, with a 1.5B binding specialist alone matching a 7B end-to-end baseline.

</details>


### [107] [An Evidence Hierarchy for Bayesian Object Classification via OSINT-Aided Heterogeneous Sensor Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22259)
*Jan Nausner,Michael Hubner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对CBRNE威胁检测，提出了一种结合OSINT语境及领域知识的贝叶斯融合框架，有效解决了传感器局限性与高背景杂波问题，实现了高达95%的分类准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对CBRNE威胁检测中单一传感器（如可靠性差异、间接指示）和环境干扰（高杂波）带来的挑战，以及高质量数据集匮乏限制学习模型发展的问题，提出一种改进的融合方案。

Method: 1. 构建新型证据层级模型，涵盖直接证据、指示性证据及背景上下文信息；2. 引入开源情报（OSINT）作为环境语境信息；3. 开发基于领域知识先验信息的贝叶斯威胁类型分类机制。

Result: 在仿真场景下验证了该方案在对抗杂波干扰和先验失配方面的鲁棒性，模型整体分类准确率最高可达95%。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的语境感知与领域知识增强的融合框架，通过证据层级建模与OSINT整合，有效提升了CBRNE威胁分类在复杂环境下的鲁棒性，克服了单一传感器局限及数据稀缺问题。

Abstract: Heterogeneous sensor fusion is vital for detecting, localizing, and classifying CBRNE threats. However, individual sensors are often only capable of detecting a subset of relevant threats with varying reliability or can even provide only indirect threat indications, making threat classification challenging. Furthermore, high clutter rates on the sensor side present a great challenge for fusion systems. Additionally, the limited availability of high quality datasets hinders the advancement of learning-based detection and classification models in smart sensors. To mitigate these sensor related shortcomings, a context-aware and domain knowledge-enhanced fusion process is proposed. First, a novel evidence hierarchy is established that enables modeling of direct, indicative, and contextual information. Second, contextual information about the environment is introduced into the fusion process, by collecting, processing, and exploiting OSINT inputs. Third, all levels of the evidence hierarchy are used to craft a Bayesian threat type classification mechanism with domain knowledge-informed priors. The proposed methodology is evaluated in simulated scenarios, and the results demonstrate the benefit of the proposed fusion approach in terms of robustness to clutter and prior mismatch, with an overall classification accuracy of up to 95%.

</details>


### [108] [PEARL: Unbiased Percentile Estimation via Contrastive Learning for Industrial-Scale Livestream Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21752)
*Blake Gella,Wei Wu,Yuhao Yin,Zexi Huang,Zikai Wang,Emily Liu,Junlin Zhang,Wentao Guo,Qinglei Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对推荐系统受用户活跃度不平衡影响的问题，本文提出了 PEARL 框架。通过建模相对偏好而非绝对交互强度，实现了无偏的百分位数偏好估计，在工业级大规模应用中显著提升了整体指标和用户体验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的推荐系统受用户行为强度不平衡（即活跃用户与沉寂用户交互模式差异巨大）的影响，导致反馈信号偏差，模型倾向于过度放大活跃用户的信号，从而损害整体推荐质量和系统的鲁棒性。

Method: 提出了一种非参数对比百分位数近似框架 PEARL。其核心利用真实对比交互样本直接近似百分位数关系，避免了辅助分布估计；引入预测引导的自助法（bootstrapping）进行百分位数平滑以处理稀疏数据，并结合广义价值加权和协同训练策略以提升模型表示学习能力。

Result: 离线实验显示 PEARL 有效缓解了行为偏差并提升了多目标的排序性能。在线 A/B 测试中，在数十亿规模的用户平台上实现了显著提升：观看时长增加 2.10%，消费金额增加 0.80%，交互率增加 1.49%，且举报率显著降低了 6.91%。

Conclusion: PEARL 通过建模相对偏好信号而非绝对交互强度，成功缓解了推荐系统中的行为强度不平衡问题，既在离线实验中表现优异，又在实战环境下取得了显著的业务指标增长，验证了该方案的有效性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Recommender systems trained on user interaction data are susceptible to behavioral intensity imbalance--a systematic distortion arising from heterogeneous engagement patterns across users. This imbalance skews feedback signals such that observed interactions no longer faithfully reflect true preferences, causing models to disproportionately amplify signals from highly active users while underrepresenting others, which ultimately degrades recommendation quality and robustness at scale. To address this issue, we propose a nonparametric contrastive percentile approximation framework, PEARL, that models relative preference signals instead of absolute engagement magnitudes. Building upon relative advantage debiasing, PEARL leverages real contrastive interaction samples to approximate percentile relationships directly, without relying on auxiliary distribution estimation models. We provide theoretical justification demonstrating that such pairwise comparisons yield unbiased estimates of percentile-based preference signals. For broader applicability, we introduce a prediction-based bootstrapping mechanism for percentile smoothing to handle sparse and discrete feedback, alongside a generalized value-weighted formulation and a co-training strategy to enhance both modeling flexibility and representation learning. Extensive offline experiments demonstrate that PEARL effectively mitigates behavioral bias and consistently improves recommendation performance across multiple ranking targets. Deployed in a production livestream platform with a combined user base of billions, online A/B testing confirms substantial real-world gains: +2.10% Watch Duration, +0.80% Consumption Amount, +1.49% Interaction Rate, and -6.91% Report Rate.
