<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨β-VAE中潜在变量“后验崩溃”（Posterior Collapse）的内在机制，并解释其与模型性能、潜空间结构之间的联系。

Method: 使用朗道稳定性分析（Landau stability analysis）对β-VAE的损失函数进行研究，定义了一个定义潜在重缩放不变的序参数，用于对激活的潜在模式进行排序和识别崩塌阈值。在线性高斯假设下，将崩塌谱与主成分分析（PCA）谱进行了对比验证，并在WorldClim数据集上进行了实验。

Result: 证明了后验崩溃本质上是一种自动化的频谱剪枝过程；在线性高斯情况下，崩塌谱、效用谱与标准化的PCA谱一致，且崩塌遵循平均场定律。

Conclusion: β-VAE通过调节β值，使潜在变量按对其重构贡献的大小从少到多发生“崩溃”，从而实现了有效的频谱剪枝。这种现象可以用朗道稳定性分析在理论上推导。

Abstract: We show that posterior collapse in $β$-VAEs implements automatic spectral pruning. A latent mode collapses if its contribution to reconstruction is below the cutoff set by $β$. Equilibrium solutions with different $β$ thus reveal a cascade of collapses as latent modes decouple from least to most useful.
  We derive this as a consequence of the loss via a Landau stability analysis. We define a latent-rescaling-invariant order parameter that ranks active latent modes and whose collapse thresholds identify which effective variables to inspect first.
  In the linear Gaussian case, the collapse spectrum, utility spectrum, and normalized PCA spectrum coincide, and each collapse follows a mean-field law. We test these predictions on the WorldClim dataset.

</details>


### [181] [Clipping Bottleneck: Stabilizing RLVR via Stochastic Recovery of Near-Boundary Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22703)
*Shuo Yang,Jinda Lu,Chiyu Ma,Kexin Huang,Haoming Meng,Qihui Zhang,Yuyang Liu,Bolin Ding,Guoyin Wang,Li Yuan,Jingren Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究发现LLM推理任务中RLVR的硬剪裁机制会导致有效信号丢失，提出了一种名为NSR的简单随机化策略，通过保留边界附近的样本显著改善了模型训练的稳定性和性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于剪裁的强化学习目标（如GRPO）中，严格的硬剪裁策略会导致训练过程中丢失处于边界附近的有效信息，从而引发训练不稳定和收敛次优的问题。

Method: 提出“近边界随机救援”（Near-boundary Stochastic Rescue, NSR）机制。该方法通过在硬剪裁阈值附近引入随机采样，有选择地保留那些处于边界外的“信息性信号”，而非简单地将其丢弃。

Result: 实验表明，在7B到30B规模的模型（包括密集和MoE架构）中，NSR相比DAPO和GSPO等强基线模型，能提供更稳定的训练过程和更优的性能表现。

Conclusion: NSR作为一种即插即用的修改方案，证明了通过随机保留边界附近的信号可以有效缓解硬剪裁带来的信息丢失，从而显著提升RLVR在大型语言模型推理中的训练稳定性和最终性能。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) has emerged as a central paradigm for scaling LLM reasoning, yet its optimization often suffers from training instability and suboptimal convergence. Through a systematic dissection of clipping-based GRPO-style objectives, we identify the rigid clipping decision induced by hard clipping as a key practical bottleneck in the studied RLVR setups. Specifically, our analysis suggests that informative signals can lie in the near-boundary region just beyond the clipping threshold, and are therefore discarded by the standard hard-clipping rule. Notably, once this bottleneck is precisely identified, even simple stochastic perturbations at the boundary can recover meaningful performance gains. Building on this finding, we propose Near-boundary Stochastic Rescue (NSR), a minimal, plug-and-play modification that stochastically retains these slightly out-of-bound tokens to recover lost signals. While NSR, via stochastic sampling, can be interpreted as inducing an implicit gradient decay in expectation, our ablations reveal that its stochastic, boundary-local rescue mechanism is consistently more effective than deterministic gradient decay. Validated by extensive experiments across model sizes from 7B to 30B and both dense and MoE architectures, as a plug-and-play solution, NSR substantially improves training stability and delivers consistent gains over strong baselines such as DAPO and GSPO.

</details>


### [182] [Abstraction for Offline Goal-Conditioned Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22711)
*Clarisse Wibault,Alexander Goldie,Antonio Villares,Maike Osborne,Jakob Foerster*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一套基于分层抽象和“相对化选项”的离线 GCRL 框架，通过在不同层级实现状态表征的抽象，有效重用了跨上下文的经验，提升了学习效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的离线目标条件强化学习（GCRL）在处理马尔可夫决策过程（MDPs）时，因状态-目标对之间的对称性和结构共享存在显著冗余，需要更有效的抽象机制来处理跨上下文的经验重用。

Method: 提出了一种利用分层结构进行绝对抽象的框架，引入了“相对化选项”及针对不同层级的独特表征，并基于此设计了两种简单的算法来学习这些相对化选项。

Result: 实验结果显示，通过引入上述归纳偏置（inductive biases），模型在离线 GCRL 任务中表现出了显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: 引入“相对化选项”（relativised options）和分层抽象能够有效处理 GCRL 中的冗余问题，通过跨上下文重用经验，显著提升了离线目标条件强化学习的性能。

Abstract: Markov Decision Processes (MDPs) often exhibit significant redundancy due to symmetries and shared structure across state-goal pairs in real-world Goal-Conditioned Reinforcement Learning (GCRL). While hierarchical policies have been motivated for horizon reduction via temporal abstraction in offline GCRL, we demonstrate that hierarchy also enables absolute abstraction. By introducing relativised options as well as distinct representations for different levels of the hierarchy, we demonstrate how an agent can reuse experience across similar contexts of the state-space. Based on this framework, we introduce two simple algorithms for learning relativised options and abstracting from the absolute frame of reference. Our experiments show that such inductive biases significantly improve performance in offline GCRL.

</details>


### [183] [Reading Task Failure Off the Activations: A Sparse-Feature Audit of GPT-2 Small on Indirect Object Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22719)
*Mahdi Nasermoghadasi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过审计GPT-2 Small在IOI任务中的表现，发现SAE特征虽能识别任务失效的相关物，但往往不具备因果驱动力，且缺乏跨随机种子的稳定性；研究强调了审计流程作为方法论的重要性，而非单一特征的发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在探讨GPT-2 Small模型在完成间接宾语识别（IOI）任务时，是否存在特定的稀疏自编码器（SAE）特征与任务执行成功或失败显著相关，并评估这些特征是否为导致行为差异的因果驱动因素。

Method: 1. 使用GPT-2 Small模型在IOI任务上进行300个提示词的测试；2. 利用Bloom (2024)的层-8残差流SAE提取特征；3. 通过Holm校正的显著性检验和Cohen's d值筛选差异特征；4. 通过因果消融（Causal ablation）、逻辑回归基准测试和多种子稳健性分析验证相关特征的因果性和稳定性。

Result: 研究发现146个特征通过统计显著性检验，其中特征17,491与“keys”对象的失效强相关（Cohen's d = 2.93）。然而，消融实验证明该特征并非失败的充分原因，且该特征仅在部分随机种子下出现，暗示其发现可能具有偶然性。逻辑回归表明SAE提升了可解释性，但未通过额外特征空间显著增强预测能力。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了一个低成本、模型无关的审计流程，用于识别与模型执行特定任务成功或失败相关的稀疏自编码器（SAE）特征。通过实证分析表明，利用SAE寻找可解释的神经相关物具有局限性：相关特征往往不能成为导致失效的充分原因，且特征的显著性可能缺乏跨种子（Seed）的稳健性。

Abstract: We report a small, reproducible audit of which sparse-autoencoder (SAE) features of GPT-2 small fire differently on failed versus successful trials of the Indirect Object Identification (IOI) task. On 300 prompts, GPT-2 small reaches 79.7% accuracy; 146 of the 24,576 features in the layer-8 residual-stream SAE release of Bloom (2024) clear a Holm-corrected significance threshold and 105 reach a large effect size (|Cohen's d| > 0.8). The strongest single correlate of failure -- feature 17,491, d=+2.93, Neuronpedia label 'cryptographic keys' -- is essentially silent except when the prompt's transferred object is 'the keys,' on which GPT-2 small fails 93.3% of the time vs. 7.5% on the other seven objects (Fisher exact p = 8.79 x 10^-33). We put this correlate through three controls that a mechanistic claim should pass. (i) A causal ablation: zeroing feature 17,491 in the residual stream across all token positions of the 45 keys prompts does not restore accuracy (6.7% -> 4.4%); the feature is a correlate, not a sufficient cause at this layer. (ii) A representation baseline: a logistic regression on the raw 768-dimensional residual stream reaches 5-fold ROC AUC = 0.929, matching the top-100 SAE features (0.927); the SAE basis adds interpretability, not predictive power. (iii) A seed-robustness check: across five random seeds the keys-subset failure rate stays in 75.0--93.3% (the behavioural effect is real), but feature 17,491 is the top-|d| feature in only 1 of 5 runs. The methodological contribution is therefore the audit pipeline (cheap, model-agnostic, surfaces named correlates) rather than any single feature found through it. We release the code, the 300-prompt corpus, the 300x24,576 activation matrix, the ablation and baseline scripts, and the figures. The full pipeline runs on a laptop (Apple M3 Max, no discrete GPU).

</details>


### [184] [The Value of Covariance Matching in Gaussian DDPMs and the Lanczos Sampler](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22723)
*Md Sahil Akhtar,Aymane El Gadarri,Vivek F. Farias,Adam D. Jozefiak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出通过匹配完整后验协方差来改进 DDPM 的采样质量，引入 Lanczos 高斯采样器 (LGS) 有效解决了计算复杂度高的问题，实现了比现有对角协方差方法更优的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准各向同性（对角）扩散模型（DDPM）在反向过程中存在不可避免的 Ω(1/T) 路径KL误差，限制了采样质量，尤其是在涉及整条路径扰动的任务（如分类器引导）中。

Method: 证明了匹配完整后验协方差可将路径KL散度降至 O(1/T²)，并提出了 Lanczos 高斯采样器 (LGS)。LGS 是一种无训练、无矩阵的方法，仅利用后验均值的雅可比-向量积进行协方差-向量乘法，实现了在不显式存储密集协方差矩阵的情况下进行高效采样。

Result: LGS 采样器的近似误差随 Lanczos 步数呈指数级衰减；实验表明仅需 3 次 Lanczos 步即可在多个基准测试中超越强对角协方差基准（如 OCM-DDPM）。

Conclusion: 完全协方差匹配不仅在理论上能够消除误差瓶颈，且通过LGS方法在实践中也是高效可行的，为实现更高质量、更少步数的扩散模型采样提供了新路径。

Abstract: A central error measure in Gaussian DDPMs is the path-space KL divergence between the exact reverse chain and the learned Gaussian reverse process. This quantity is especially relevant for procedures such as classifier guidance, which perturb the entire reverse trajectory rather than only the terminal sample. Prior analyses show that standard isotropic reverse covariances suffer an unavoidable $Ω(1/T)$ path-KL error as the number of denoising steps $T$ grows. We show that matching the full posterior covariance breaks this barrier, yielding an order-wise improvement that reduces the path KL to $O(1/T^2)$. To make full covariance matching practical, we introduce the Lanczos Gaussian sampler (LGS), a training-free, matrix-free method for sampling from the optimal reverse covariance using only covariance-vector products, which are available through Jacobian-vector products of the posterior mean. LGS avoids dense covariance storage and auxiliary covariance models. We prove that LGS approximation error decays exponentially in the number of Lanczos steps, where each Lanczos step requires a single Jacobian-vector product. Empirically, using only just three such steps improves sample quality over strong diagonal-covariance baselines, including OCM-DDPM, across standard image benchmarks. This identifies full covariance matching as both theoretically valuable and practically accessible for fast DDPM sampling.

</details>


### [185] [SeqLoRA: Bilevel Orthogonal Adaptation for Continual Multi-Concept Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22743)
*Javad Parsa,Enis Simsar,Amir Joudaki,Thomas Hofmann,André M. H. Teixeira*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 SeqLoRA，通过双层优化联合调整 LoRA 参数，有效解决了多概念个性化生成中的表征干扰和遗忘问题，实现了高达 101 个概念的高保真扩展，且无需额外的后处理融合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的文本到图像模型个性化微调方法在组合多个概念时存在“表征干扰”问题。现有的模块化方法要么依赖昂贵的后处理融合，要么冻结适应子空间，从而限制了模型的表达能力和概念保真度。

Method: 提出 SeqLoRA（Sequential regularized LoRA），这是一种基于双层优化（bilevel optimization）的受限持续学习框架，旨在联合优化 LoRA 权重矩阵。通过将残差层激活建模为矩阵次高斯过程（matrix sub-Gaussian process）来限制灾难性遗忘，并证明了学习 LoRA 基比冻结基方法更能降低残差干扰能量。

Result: 实验表明，SeqLoRA 在多概念图像生成中显著提升了身份保持能力，并成功扩展至 101 个概念，同时有效减少了属性干扰。

Conclusion: SeqLoRA 提供了一种有效的多概念文本到图像生成方案，在保持高性能的同时扩展性极强，且无需昂贵的后处理融合。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning enables fast personalization of text-to-image diffusion models, but composing multiple custom concepts remains challenging due to representation interference. Existing modular methods either rely on expensive post-hoc fusion or freeze adaptation subspaces, which limit expressiveness and concept fidelity. To address this trade-off, we propose Sequential regularized LoRA (SeqLoRA), a constrained continual learning framework that jointly optimizes both LoRA factors via bilevel optimization. Theoretically, we establish strong convergence guarantees for our algorithm and model the residual layer activations as a matrix sub-Gaussian process to derive high-probability bounds on catastrophic forgetting. We further prove that learning the LoRA basis from data minimizes residual interference energy more effectively than frozen-basis methods. Experiments on multi-concept image generation demonstrate that SeqLoRA improves identity preservation and scalability across up to 101 concepts, while avoiding costly fusion and reducing attribute interference in composed generations.

</details>


### [186] [CogAdapt: Transferring Clinical ECG Foundation Models to Wearable Cognitive Load Assessment via Lead Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22774)
*Amir Mousavi,Mohammad Sadegh Sirjani,Erfan Nourbakhsh,Mimi Xie,Rocky Slavin,Leslie Neely,John Davis,John Quarles*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CogAdapt 框架通过适配器架构和渐进式微调技术，将临床级大规模预训练的心电模型成功迁移至可穿戴设备的认知负荷评估任务，实现了优异的跨被试性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现实时的可穿戴认知负荷评估，但现有研究受限于有标签数据稀缺及跨被试泛化能力差，且现有的医疗级心电（ECG）基础模型因传感器配置（导联数）和任务差异无法直接部署于可穿戴设备。

Method: 提出了 CogAdapt 框架，包含两个核心模块：1. LeadBridge：一个可学习的适配器，将可穿戴的 3 导联 ECG 信号映射为解剖学上一致的 12 导联临床表征；2. ProFine：一种渐进式微调策略，通过逐层解冻编码器并引入防止灾难性遗忘的机制，实现高效的模型适应。

Result: 在 CLARE 和 CL-Drive 两个数据集上进行留一法交叉验证（LOSO），CogAdapt 的宏 F1 分数分别达到 0.626 和 0.768，显著优于从头训练的基准模型。

Conclusion: CogAdapt 通过结合跨导联转换与渐进式微调，有效解决了可穿戴设备认知负荷评估中数据匮乏及跨被试泛化性差的问题，证明了将通用心电基础模型迁移至特定任务的可行性与优越性。

Abstract: Real-time cognitive load assessment is essential for adaptive human-computer interaction but remains challenging due to limited labeled data and poor cross-subject generalization. Recent ECG foundation models pre-trained on millions of clinical recordings offer rich representations, but cannot be directly applied to wearable devices due to sensor configuration mismatch and task differences. In this paper, we propose CogAdapt, a framework that adapts clinical ECG foundation models to wearable cognitive load assessment. CogAdapt introduces LeadBridge, a learnable adapter that transforms 3-lead wearable signals into anatomically consistent 12-lead representations, and ProFine, a progressive fine-tuning strategy that gradually unfreezes encoder layers while preventing catastrophic forgetting. Evaluations on two public datasets (CLARE and CL-Drive) under leave-one-subject-out cross-validation show that CogAdapt substantially outperforms baselines trained from scratch, achieving macro-F1 scores of 0.626 and 0.768. These results demonstrate the promise of foundation model adaptation for subject-independent cognitive load assessment from wearable sensors.

</details>


### [187] [Remember to be Curious: Episodic Context and Persistent Worlds for 3D Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22814)
*Lily Goli,Justin Kerr,Daniele Reda,Alec Jacobson,Andrea Tagliasacchi,Angjoo Kanazawa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对复杂长程任务中好奇心驱动学习的低效问题，通过引入在线三维重建与序列观察建模，赋予智能体空间持久性和情景记忆，从而实现在稀疏奖励下的高效自主探索与泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有好奇心驱动的强化学习方法在复杂、逼真的三维环境中容易陷入局部循环，过度关注遗忘记忆的旧状态，缺乏空间持久性和情景感知能力。

Method: 提出了一种结合在线三维重建（作为持久的世界模型）与序列模型（作为策略，处理RGB观测以维护情景上下文）的框架，通过好奇心驱动的内生奖励进行训练。

Result: 在HM3D数据集上，该方法在完全好奇心驱动的学习下，性能优于基于RL的主动映射基准；在Gibson及AI生成场景中表现出良好的零样本泛化能力；在下游任务（苹果采摘、图像目标导航）中显著优于从零开始的学习基准。

Conclusion: 通过构建持久的三维世界模型并引入序列建模来维持情景记忆，该研究有效解决了好奇心驱动强化学习在复杂三维环境下的“局部循环”和“奖励伪影”问题，实现了高效的零样本泛化和下游任务适配。

Abstract: Exploration is a prerequisite for learning useful behaviors in sparse-reward, long-horizon tasks, particularly within 3D environments. Curiosity-driven reinforcement learning addresses this via intrinsic rewards derived from the mismatch between the agent's predictive model of the world and reality. However, translating this intrinsic motivation to complex, photorealistic environments remains difficult, as agents can become trapped in local loops and receive fresh rewards for revisiting forgotten states. In this work, we demonstrate that this failure stems from a lack of spatial persistence and episodic context. We show that effective curiosity requires a model of the world that is persistent and continuously updated, paired with an agent that maintains an episodic trajectory history to navigate toward novel regions. We achieve this using an online 3D reconstruction as a persistent model of the world, while the agent policy is parameterized as a sequence model over RGB observations to maintain episodic context. This design enables effective exploration during training while allowing the agent to navigate using solely RGB frames at deployment. Trained purely via curiosity on HM3D, our agent outperforms RL-based active mapping baselines and generalizes zero-shot to Gibson and AI-generated worlds. Our end-to-end policy enables efficient adaptation to downstream tasks, such as apple picking and image-goal navigation, outperforming from-scratch baselines. Please see video results at https://recuriosity.github.io/.

</details>


<div id='cs.DS'></div>

# cs.DS [[Back]](#toc)

### [188] [Near-Optimal Generalized Private Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21601)
*Anamay Chaturvedi,Monika Henzinger,Jalaj Upadhyay*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出了广义阈值机制（GTM），解决了差分隐私序列机制的评估问题，为持续观测环境下的 DP 优化提供了高效的黑盒归约工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决在差分隐私环境下处理序列机制评估的广义私有测试问题，并弥合在线持续观察（CO）设置与批处理设置之间的差距，以改进超参数优化和最大化问题的算法。

Method: 提出了广义阈值机制（Generalized Thresholding Mechanism, GTM），通过在满足纯 $\varepsilon$-差分隐私的同时，实现对任意 $(\varepsilon_t, \delta_t)$-DP 机制序列的自动测试，并能够根据准确度要求自适应调整接受阈值。

Result: GTM 实现了纯 $\varepsilon$-DP，并给出了明确的准确度边界（$\bar{p}_t$）和样本复杂度上限（$O(\text{poly}(\ln t, 1/\beta, 1/\text{gap}))$），证明了其近乎最优性。此外，该机制成功应用到 DP-CO 优化中，并解决了超参数优化中自适应阈值的难题。

Conclusion: GTM 机制在广义私有测试问题上实现了近乎最优的准确度和样本复杂度，为差分隐私优化提供了一种通用的黑盒归约方法，并支持自适应阈值选择。

Abstract: In differential privacy (DP), the generalized private testing problem was introduced by Liu and Talwar (STOC 2019). Given a dataset $X \in \mathcal{X}$ and a sequence of black-box $\varepsilon_t$-DP mechanisms $M_t:\mathcal{X}\to\{+1,-1\}$, the analyst must accept the first mechanism whose success probability $p_t=\Pr[M_t(X)=+1]$ exceeds a given threshold $p^*\in(0,1)$, while achieving DP. Accuracy is measured by the gap between $p^*$ and a rejection threshold $\bar{p}$, such that with probability $1-β$ for all $t\geq1$, if $p_t\leq\bar{p}$, then $M_t$ is rejected, and if $p_t\geq p^*$, then it is accepted. This generalizes the standard private testing problem, whose solution, the Sparse Vector Technique, is ubiquitous in DP.
  We introduce the Generalized Thresholding Mechanism (GTM) for generalized private testing. For $\varepsilon>0$ and any sequence of $(\varepsilon_t,δ_t)$-DP mechanisms $M_t$, the GTM is pure $\varepsilon$-DP. For $θ>0$, $γ\in(1,2]$, and $β\in(0,1)$, $\bar{p}_t=\max(p^*/γΛ_t, 1 - γΛ_t(1-p^*))-δ_t/\varepsilon_t$ for $Λ_t=(5t\ln^3(t+2))^{(2+θ)\varepsilon_t/\varepsilon}(4/β)^{(3+θ+2/θ)\varepsilon_t/\varepsilon}$. With probability $1-β$, the number of evaluations of $M_t$ is at most $O((\ln(t/β)/(γ-1)^2)\max(Λ_t/p^*,(1-p^*)^{-1}))$ for all $t\geq 1$. Our lower bounds prove near-optimality of our accuracy and sample complexity guarantees.
  Via the GTM, we give a black-box reduction for DP optimization from the continual observation (CO) setting to the batch setting. This gives us the first DP-CO algorithms for many maximization problems. Further, the GTM permits an adaptive choice of acceptance thresholds $(p^*_t)_{t\geq1}$, addressing a challenge mentioned in prior work on using generalized private testing for hyperparameter optimization (Papernot and Steinke (ICLR 2022)).

</details>


### [189] [Finding a Solution to the Erdős-Ginzburg-Ziv Theorem in Linear Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21753)
*Sunghyeon Jo*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 该论文提出了解决 Erdős-Ginzburg-Ziv 定理中子序列查找问题的首个线性时间确定性算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管 Erdős-Ginzburg-Ziv 定理已被证明，且已有 O(n log n) 和 O(n log log log n) 的算法，但仍存在进一步优化该算法时间复杂度的空间，目标是实现线性时间复杂度。

Method: 提出了一个针对素数模的子集和问题的线性时间算法，核心思想是利用算术级数（Arithmetic Progression）的紧凑表示。当级数相交时，通过受限的 Frobenius 区间进行合并，确保足够的增长以平摊更新成本。对于一般模数，则通过标准的乘法约化扩展到该素数算法。

Result: 实现了一个确定性的线性时间算法来寻找 Erdős-Ginzburg-Ziv 定理承诺的子序列。

Conclusion: 作者证明了寻找满足条件的子序列可以在线性时间内完成，标志着该问题的计算复杂性达到了最优。

Abstract: The Erdős-Ginzburg-Ziv theorem states that every sequence of 2n - 1 integers contains a subsequence of length n whose sum is divisible by n. Choi, Kang, and Lim gave a simple deterministic O(n log n) algorithm for finding such a subsequence, and Leung recently improved this to O(n log log log n). We give a deterministic linear-time algorithm. The core is a linear-time algorithm for the following prime target subset-sum problem: given p - 1 nonzero residues in Z_p and a target residue, find a subset with the prescribed sum. Our algorithm maintains a compact arithmetic-progression representation of reachable sums. When two progressions intersect, a bounded Frobenius interval in their sum allows them to be merged into one longer progression, with enough growth to pay for the update. When the representation either contains a full progression or covers all nonzero residues, the target residue is recovered constructively. The standard multiplicative reduction then extends the prime algorithm to arbitrary moduli.

</details>


### [190] [An $Ω(n \log n)$ Randomized Lower Bound for Cutting a Cake into Proportionally Fair Pieces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21829)
*Stephen Arndt,Kirk Pruhs,Trung Tran*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 该研究在Robertson-Webb模型下，从理论上证明了实现比例公平分配的随机算法至少需要 $\Omega(n \log n)$ 次查询。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决公平分配问题中，评估实现特定公平准则（如比例公平）所需的最小资源（查询次数）消耗问题。

Method: 理论分析与复杂性证明，利用Robertson-Webb模型的查询机制分析随机算法的下界。

Result: 证明了在随机算法框架下，比例公平分配至少需要 $\Omega(n \log n)$ 次查询。

Conclusion: 在Robertson-Webb模型中，为了实现比例公平（proportional fairness），任何随机算法的查询复杂度下界为 $\Omega(n \log n)$。

Abstract: We consider the classic cake cutting problem in the Robertson-Webb model, with the objective of proportional fairness. We show that any randomized algorithm must use $Ω(n \log n)$ queries.

</details>


### [191] [Optimal $e^{(γ+o(1))n}$-Approximation of the Permanent of Positive Semidefinite Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21946)
*Nima Anari,Farzam Ebrahimnejad*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文确定了厄米半正定矩阵积和式的确定性多项式时间近似比的最优指数界为 $e^{\gamma n}$，该结论通过熵论证方法证明并在理论上达到了最优解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在寻找厄米半正定矩阵积和式在确定性多项式时间内的最佳近似算法，并确定其理论极限。

Method: 使用熵论证方法（entropy argument），结合 Wick 积分公式，构造并证明了积和式的上下界“夹心”不等式（sandwiching estimate），通过凸优化求解 $\widehat P(A)$。

Result: 证明了对于 $n \times n$ 厄米半正定矩阵 $A$，存在 $e^{-\gamma n}\widehat P(A) \le \operatorname{per}(A) \le \widehat P(A)$ 的界，利用凸优化可实现 $e^{(\gamma+\epsilon)n}$ 的近似，并给出了相应的硬度界。

Conclusion: 确定了厄米半正定矩阵积和式（Permanent）在确定性多项式时间内的最佳逼近比为 $e^{(\gamma+o(1))n}$（其中 $\gamma$ 为欧拉-马斯刻若尼常数），在 $\mathrm{P}\ne\mathrm{NP}$ 的假设下，该结果在指数级上达到了最优。

Abstract: We determine, up to lower-order terms in the exponent, the best possible deterministic polynomial-time approximation ratio for the permanent of a Hermitian positive semidefinite matrix. If $A\succeq 0$ has no zero diagonal entry, $d=\operatorname{rank}(A)$, $A=VV^\dagger$ with $V\in\mathbb{C}^{n\times d}$ full column rank, and $v_1,\ldots,v_n$ are the rows of $V$, define \[
  Φ(V)=\max_{X\succ 0}
  \left\{\sum_{i=1}^n \log(v_i^\dagger Xv_i)+\log\det X-\operatorname{tr} X+d\right\},
  \qquad
  \widehat P(A)=e^{Φ(V)}. \] We prove the exact sandwich \[
  e^{-γn}\widehat P(A)\le \operatorname{per}(A)\le \widehat P(A). \] Here $γ$ is the Euler--Mascheroni constant. Since the maximization is concave, this gives a deterministic polynomial-time $e^{(γ+\varepsilon)n}$-approximation for every $\varepsilon>0$. Combined with the previous $e^{(γ-\varepsilon)n}$-hardness of approximation for positive semidefinite permanents, this resolves the optimal exponential approximation ratio for deterministic polynomial-time algorithms as $e^{(γ+o(1))n}$, assuming $\mathrm{P}\ne\mathrm{NP}$. The proof is an entropy argument applied to the standard Wick integral formula for $\operatorname{per}(A)$; the loss is exactly $γ$ per factor because $\mathbb{E}[\log T]=-γ$ for $T\sim\operatorname{Exp}(1)$.
  The result was obtained through interactions with GPT 5.5 Pro Extended: the first author's interaction was one-shot, while the second author's was a separate multi-turn interaction with high-level guidance. Both authors verified the theorem and proof. Codex was used to assemble and typeset the manuscript.

</details>


### [192] [A Coalgebraic Dijkstra Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22149)
*Takahiro Sanada,Yoàv Montacute,Kittiphon Phalakarn,Ichiro Hasuo*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出了“代数协同最短路径问题”（CSPP）框架，通过代数协同方法统一了多种路径优化问题，并给出了Dijkstra算法适用的充要条件及高效求解方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Dijkstra算法及其变体（如最宽路径问题、双人游戏等）缺乏统一的数学描述和一般性处理框架，作者旨在建立一个能够覆盖这些问题以及新问题（如最短二叉树问题）的统一数学框架，并探讨Dijkstra式算法加速的适用准则。

Method: 构建了代数协同最短路径问题（CSPP）框架，利用协同代数（coalgebraic）方法抽象状态转移系统，并基于此设计了通用的Dijkstra类算法，通过分析算法正确性的必要与充分条件来界定Dijkstra加速的适用范围。

Result: 成功建立了CSPP框架；确定了Dijkstra算法正确性的充要条件；证明了提出的算法能够通过统一的视角解决多种复杂优化问题，且保持了与经典Dijkstra算法相当的渐近复杂度。

Conclusion: 提出了一个通用的代数协同（Coalgebraic）Dijkstra算法框架，并通过该框架定义了Dijkstra类算法适用的必要且充分条件，证明了在特定条件下该方法的异步复杂度与经典Dijkstra算法相当，实现了对多种最短路径类问题的统一处理。

Abstract: The Dijkstra algorithm is a classical method for solving the shortest path problem on weighted graphs. There are several variations of the Dijkstra algorithm, including algorithms for the widest path problem and for two-player games. In this paper, we introduce the coalgebraic shortest path problem (CSPP), a unifying framework for a broad class of optimization problems on state-transition systems. This framework encompasses not only the aforementioned problems but also new ones such as the shortest binary tree problem. We further present a coalgebraic Dijkstra algorithm for solving the CSPP efficiently under a suitable condition. Our condition is necessary and sufficient for the algorithm to return correct solutions, thereby providing a precise criterion for when Dijkstra-style acceleration is possible. We also show that the proposed algorithm achieves asymptotic complexity comparable to that of the classical Dijkstra algorithm.

</details>


### [193] [The Secretary Problem with a Stochastic Precursor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22653)
*Franziska Eberle,Alexander Lindermayr*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新型“学习增强”机制，证明了在线算法中“信号到来的时间点”本身就是一种无需内容即可显著提升决策性能的有效建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的学习增强（learning-augmented）在线算法通常依赖于预测的具体内容，本文旨在探索“信号的到达时间”这一纯时间维度信息是否具备预测价值及能力。

Method: 研究了带有随机前体（content-free signal）的秘书问题。该信号不携带任何信息，仅在最优项目之前到达，通过数学分析在随机排布模型（random-order）和对抗排布模型（adversarial-order）下推导最优策略。

Result: 在随机排布模型中，即使是简单的均匀前体信号也能将成功概率从经典的1/e提升至至少1/2，且随信号延迟增加，成功率趋近于1；在对抗排布模型中，只要前体信号足够集中，也能实现常数级的成功概率保证。

Conclusion: 异步时间信息（即“前体信号”的到达时间）本身就是一种功能强大的在线决策建议形式，无需包含任何内容信息即可显著提高算法性能。

Abstract: In learning-augmented online algorithms, predictions are usually valued for what they say: a value estimate, a solution, or an algorithmic recommendation. This paper shows that predictions can also be valuable solely due to their arrival time. We study the fundamental secretary problem augmented with a stochastic precursor: a content-free signal that is guaranteed to arrive no later than the best item, but is otherwise stochastically timed. The signal does not carry any additional information; nevertheless, its timing alone changes the structure of optimal stopping. We characterize optimal policies in the random-order and adversarial-order models. In random order, a single uniformly timed precursor already gives success probability at least $\frac12$, improving on the classic $\frac1e$ benchmark. With increasingly late precursors, the success probability approaches $1$. In adversarial order, for which traditional models do not admit strong guarantees, sufficiently concentrated precursors recover constant success guarantees. Our results show that such novel forms of asynchronous temporal information are a distinct and powerful form of advice in online decision making and may also be effective for other problems.

</details>


### [194] [On the Parameterized Complexity of Min-Sum-Radii](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22804)
*Pankaj Kumar,Haiko Müller,Sebastian Ordyniak,Melanie Schmidt*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 该研究揭示了最小半径和（MSR）聚类问题在多种图结构参数下的计算复杂性，证明了其在多数典型结构参数下是W[1]-难的，但确认了在“树宽+聚类代价”这一联合参数下是固定参数可解（FPT）的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨在图度量空间下，最小半径和（MSR）聚类问题的参数化复杂性，特别是回答关于树宽参数下是否从XP提升至FPT的问题，并探索其他结构参数下的硬度。

Method: 通过计算复杂性理论的归约方法（W[1]-hardness证明）以及算法设计（基于树宽的FPT算法）来评估MSR问题的参数化复杂度。

Result: 1. MSR在加权二分图上的联合参数（k+Delta）及（顶点覆盖数+k）下均为W[1]-难；2. 在完全图和完全二分图上，受（k+Delta）参数约束下为W[1]-难；3. 证明了以（树宽+Delta）为参数时，MSR是固定参数可解（FPT）的。

Conclusion: 该研究确定了最小半径和（MSR）聚类问题在图度量下的参数化复杂性界限：对于加权图，该问题在受限拓扑下（如二分图、团）通常是W[1]-难的；但在树宽与聚类代价（Delta）的联合参数下是固定参数可解（FPT）的。

Abstract: In the Min-Sum-Radii (MSR) clustering problem, we are given a finite set X of n points in a metric space. The objective is to find at most k clusters centered at a subset of these points such that every point of X is assigned to one of the clusters, minimizing the sum of the radii of the clusters. The problem is known to be NP-hard even on metrics induced by weighted planar graphs and metrics with constant doubling dimension, as shown by Gibson et al. (SWAT 2008). In this work, we investigate the parameterized complexity of MSR on metrics induced by undirected graphs. We distinguish between weighted graph metrics (with positive edge weights) and unweighted graph metrics (where all edges have unit weight).
  Weighted Graph Metrics: We show that MSR is W[1]-hard on metrics induced by weighted bipartite graphs, when parameterized by the combined parameter k (the number of clusters) and Delta (the cost of the clustering). We then investigate the structural parameterized complexity of the problem. Drexler et al. (arXiv:2310.02130) showed that the MSR problem admits an XP algorithm on metrics induced by weighted graphs when parameterized by treewidth, and asked whether this can be improved to fixed-parameter tractability. We first answer their question in the negative, and more strongly show that MSR stays W[1]-hard on metrics induced by undirected weighted bipartite graphs when parameterized by the vertex cover number plus k. We then turn our attention to parameters for dense graphs and show that MSR remains W[1]-hard when parameterized by k+Delta even on cliques and complete bipartite graphs. On the positive side, we employ the known XP algorithm parameterized by treewidth, to show that the MSR problem is FPT when parameterized by the parameter treewidth plus Delta.

</details>


### [195] [Optimal Testing of Reed-Muller Codes with an Online Adversary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22813)
*Esty Kelman,Uri Meir,Kai Zhe Zheng*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文引入了“半样本测试器”来解决 Reed-Muller 码在在线擦除模型下的测试难题，通过子集采样策略实现了最优的查询复杂度，并将其扩展至提升仿射不变码，有效提升了抗擦除攻击的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了应对在线擦除模型（online-erasure model），即在查询过程中存在攻击者会对输入函数进行擦除，使得传统的测试查询模式显得过于可预测。需在保持低查询复杂度的同时，增强测试机制对擦除攻击的鲁棒性。

Method: 定义并分析了“半样本测试器（semi-sample-based testers）”，将其作为样本测试器（随机查询）和标准测试器（自由选择查询）的混合体。该测试器先选择域的一个子集 S，然后在 S 内进行均匀随机查询。

Result: 实现了 Reed-Muller 码在在线擦除模型下的最优查询复杂度，优于 Minzer 和 Zheng (2024) 的前期工作，并为提升仿射不变码提供了首个在线擦除模型下的测试方案。

Conclusion: 研究证明了半样本测试器在在线擦除模型下对 Reed-Muller 码的有效性，实现了查询复杂度的最优解，并首次将该测试器扩展到了提升仿射不变码（lifted affine-invariant codes）。

Abstract: Motivated by applications to property testing in the online-erasure model of Kalemaj, Raskhodnikova, and Varma (ITCS 2022 and Theory of Computing 2023), we define and analyze {\em semi-sample-based testers} for Reed-Muller codes. The task in Reed-Muller testing is to determine whether an input function $f: \F^n \to \F$ belongs to the Reed-Muller code or is far from it, using as few point queries to $f$ as possible. Reed-Muller testing is a well-studied task with its roots in both the Property Testing and Probabilistically Checkable Proofs literature. The online-erasure model introduces a twist: after each query made, an adversary may erase up to $t$ points of the input function, potentially thwarting any test in which the queries follow a predictable pattern.
  Semi-sample-based testers are a hybrid between sample-based testers -- which can only make uniformly random queries to the input function -- and standard testers, which can choose their queries freely. They are designed with the online-erasure model in mind and operate by first choosing some subset $S$ of the domain and then making their queries uniformly at random inside of $S$. We describe semi-sample-based testers for the Reed-Muller code and give an optimal analysis of their soundness.
  Consequently, we show that semi-sample-based testers are indeed effective in the presence of online erasures, and thereby achieve optimal query complexity for testing the Reed-Muller code in the online-erasure model. This result improves upon prior work of Minzer and Zheng (SODA 2024). As an added bonus, we show that semi-sample-based testers also exist for the lifted affine-invariant codes of Guo, Kopparty, and Sudan (ITCS 2013), thereby providing the first known testers for these codes in the online-erasure model.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [196] [Adaptive RBF-KAN: A Comparative Evaluation of Dynamic Shape Parameters in Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21534)
*Roberto Cavoretto,Alessandra De Rossi,Adeeba Haider,Amir Noorizadegan*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了Adaptive RBF-KAN，通过引入Matérn和Wendland核函数并利用LOOCV进行数据驱动的参数初始化，有效克服了FastKAN灵活性不足的问题，提升了模型对多种函数类型的拟合效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原版KAN采用B-splines计算开销大；FastKAN虽使用RBF提高了效率，但受限于固定的高斯核及其预设的形状参数，适应性不足。

Method: 1. 扩展RBF核系列：引入了Matérn和Wendland核函数；2. 自适应初始化：利用留一法交叉验证（LOOCV）对核函数的形状参数进行数据驱动的初始化；3. 参数微调：在模型训练阶段持续优化核函数的尺度参数。

Result: 在多个二维基准函数上的实验表明，该方法能够根据函数特性（如平滑性或不连续性）匹配合适的核函数，且自适应形状参数显著提升了模型的表示能力和预测表现。

Conclusion: 引入LOOCV进行核尺度初始化，并结合Matérn和Wendland核函数，能有效提升RBF-KAN处理不同函数类型（平滑、不连续、震荡）的灵活性与性能，是优化RBF基KAN模型的实用策略。

Abstract: Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (KANs) approximate multivariate functions using learnable univariate edge functions, typically parameterized by B-spline bases. Although effective, spline-based implementations can be computationally expensive. A modified version of KANs, called FastKAN, improves efficiency by replacing splines with Gaussian radial basis functions (RBFs), but it relies on a fixed kernel and shape parameter. In this work, we extend the RBF-based KAN framework by introducing a broader family of radial basis kernels and by initializing the kernel shape parameter using leave-one-out cross-validation (LOOCV). To the best of our knowledge, this is the first study that integrates LOOCV-based kernel scale estimation with deep KAN training. We also introduce Matérn and Wendland kernels into the KAN framework for the first time, enabling more flexible basis representations beyond the Gaussian kernel used in FastKAN. The LOOCV estimate provides a data-driven initialization of the kernel scale, which is subsequently refined during network training. The proposed adaptive RBF-KAN is evaluated on several two-dimensional benchmark functions. The results highlight the importance of kernel selection and adaptive shape parameters, with different kernels showing advantages for smooth functions, discontinuities, and oscillatory patterns. Overall, combining LOOCV-based initialization with adaptive kernel learning provides a practical strategy for improving RBF-based KAN models.

</details>


### [197] [Local Covariate Selection for Average Causal Effect Estimation without Pretreatment and Causal Sufficiency Assumptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21548)
*Zeyu Liu,Zheng Li,Feng Xie,Yan Zeng,Hao Zhang,Kun Zhang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种局部因果协变量选择算法，克服了现有因果推断方法依赖严苛假设和计算开销大的问题，实现了局部范围内的完备性与高效估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖于过于严苛的假设（如因果充分性、预处理假设）或计算昂贵的全局结构学习，在高维数据场景下难以应用。

Method: 提出了一种基于“局部边界”（local boundary）的方法；通过定义包含有效调整集的局部区域，利用局部识别程序在该边界内高效搜索，避免了全局因果结构学习。

Result: 在合成数据集和真实数据集上的实验结果表明，该方法在保持因果效应估计准确性的同时，相比现有方法显著提高了计算效率。

Conclusion: 提出了一种新的局部学习算法，在无需传统预处理假设或因果充分性假设的情况下，能够有效且完备地进行因果效应估计的协变量选择。

Abstract: We study the problem of selecting covariates for unbiased estimation of the total causal effect.Existing approaches typically rely on global causal structure learning over all variables, or on strong assumptions such as causal sufficiency - where observed variables share no latent confounders - or the pretreatment assumption, which limits covariates to those unaffected by the treatment or outcome. These requirements are often unrealistic in practice, and global learning becomes computationally prohibitive in high-dimensional settings.To address these challenges, we propose a novel local learning method for covariate selection in nonparametric causal effect estimation that avoids both the pretreatment and causal sufficiency assumptions. We first characterize a local boundary that contains at least one valid adjustment set whenever one exists for identifying the causal effect, and then develop local identification procedures to efficiently search within this boundary.We prove that the proposed method is sound and complete. Experiments on multiple synthetic datasets and two real-world datasets show that our approach achieves accurate causal effect estimation while substantially improving computational efficiency.

</details>


### [198] [Support-aware offline policy selection for advertising marketplaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21736)
*Prashant Shekhar,Caroline Howard*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 针对广告底价离线评估风险高的问题，本文提出了一种支持敏感的决策框架，通过输出认证的策略子集而非单一模型，有效平衡了收益提升与统计风险控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的在线广告底价离线评估方法（如回放表）虽然能发现高收益策略，但存在弱支持、多重比较问题、子群危害及竞标者响应不确定性等风险，缺乏一个能够判断现有证据是否足以支撑策略验证的决策标准。

Method: 开发了一个支持敏感的（support-aware）离线决策框架。该框架将日志证据转化为包含“认证策略”、“统计劣势方案”和“待验证候选者”的决策对象；利用有限目录保证（finite-catalog guarantee）并在同时性不确定性控制和保守支持门控下运行。

Result: 在iPinYou数据集上的实验表明，该框架能将19个策略缩减为2个验证清单，同时在44个细分维度上认证了无风险，最终实验策略实现了显著的离线回放提升（40.71%-47.66%）。

Conclusion: 离线广告底价策略评估应从单一的点估计排序转向提供认证的验证决策，以降低风险并确保策略表现的统计鲁棒性。

Abstract: Logged advertising auctions make offline reserve-price evaluation attractive but risky. Replay tables can identify policies with large apparent yield gains, yet they can also hide weak threshold support, multiple-comparison effects, subgroup harm, and bidder-response uncertainty. Existing replay and off-policy evaluation methods estimate or rank policy values, but they do not directly answer the operational question of whether the available evidence is strong enough to justify validation. This paper develops a support-aware offline decision framework for reserve-policy selection. Rather than outputting a single point-estimate winner, the framework converts logged evidence into a conservative decision object consisting of certified policies, statistically dominated alternatives, and unresolved candidates requiring further validation. The main theoretical result gives a unified finite-catalog guarantee showing that, under simultaneous uncertainty control and conservative support gates, the framework preserves the best gate-passing policy while eliminating only policies with certified regret. Supporting results characterize support-localized replay generalization, establish information-theoretic threshold-resolution limits, and quantify when heterogeneous bidder response can overturn localized replay rankings. Experiments on iPinYou real-time-bidding logs show that the leading reserve rule achieves a 47.66% replay lift in season two, a 40.71% simultaneous lower-bound lift, and a 43.87% frozen out-of-time replay lift in season three. The framework reduces a 19-policy catalog to a two-policy validation shortlist while certifying non-harm across 44 advertiser, exchange, and region segments. The results support the central claim that offline reserve-policy evaluation should produce certified validation decisions rather than point-estimate rankings alone.

</details>


### [199] [From Betting to Empirical Bernstein LIL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22124)
*Francesco Orabona*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该技术报告通过在线博弈策略的财富增长保证，为重对数律（LIL）提供了一种基于博弈论的推导证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在通过博弈论框架重新审视经典概率论中的重对数律，即利用在线博弈策略的财富约束来推导该定理。

Method: 利用在线博弈策略（Online Betting Strategy）的财富演化过程作为分析工具，推导并证明了重对数律。

Result: 成功建立了一套基于博弈策略财富分析的重对数律推导方法，体现了博弈论概率论的实用价值。

Conclusion: 本文通过在线博弈策略的财富保证成功导出了重对数律（Law of the Iterated Logarithm, LIL），验证了博弈论在概率论基础研究中的有效性。

Abstract: This is a verbatim copy of a technical report I wrote in 2017-2018 to obtain the law of the iterated logarithm using the guarantee on the wealth of an online betting strategy.

</details>


### [200] [Do Not Trust The Auctioneer: Learning to Bid in Feedback-Manipulated Auctions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22438)
*Luigi Foscari,Matilde Tullii,Vianney Perchet*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了重复一价拍卖中“托标”对学习策略的影响。虽然托标不改变分配结果，但它通过污染反馈数据增加了学习难度。作者设计了一种结合鲁邦与乐观策略的混合算法，实现了最优收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在重复一价拍卖中，托标（Shilling）行为虽不影响拍卖结果（分配），但会扭曲学习者对真实竞价分布的观察（反馈），从而干扰学习者对最优竞价策略的选择。作者旨在研究这种仅影响反馈的操纵如何增加学习的难度。

Method: 提出了一种结合两种策略的算法：1. 鲁棒区间消除分支（Robust interval-elimination）：忽略托标报告以实现动态定价速率 $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{2/3})$；2. 乐观分支（Optimistic branch）：对失败报告进行去偏处理，在信息可靠时实现 $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(\sqrt{T})$ 的一价拍卖速率。使用验证与竞速过程（Validation and racing）在两者间自动切换。

Result: 在单活跃区域情况下，该算法实现了与理论下界匹配的 regret（对数因子除外），证明即使不改变分配结果，托标行为也会显著增加学习难度。

Conclusion: 仅限反馈的托标行为（Shilling）会显著改变重复拍卖中学习的统计难度，即使是在托标分布已知的情况下，算法也需要复杂的策略来平衡信息的可靠性与学习效率。

Abstract: Shilling is the use of artificial bids to make competition appear stronger and push prices upward. We study repeated first-price auctions in which shilling affects feedback but not allocation: the learner wins or loses against the real competing bid, but after a loss observes the maximum of the real bid and an independent shill bid. Thus the manipulation changes what the learner observes and hence how it learns to bid, without changing the outcome of the current auction. We analyze regret with respect to the best bid benchmark, assuming that the shill-bid distribution is known. Even then, shilling can mask the real bid, while useful side information appears only through intermittent low-shill events. Our algorithm combines a robust interval-elimination branch, which ignores the shilled report and achieves the dynamic-pricing rate $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{2/3})$, with an optimistic branch that debiases losing-side reports and exploits the resulting suffix information when it is reliable and achieves the first-price auctions rate $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(\sqrt{T})$. A validation and racing procedure lets the algorithm use these optimistic updates without knowing the right scale or feedback geometry in advance. We complement the upper bounds with a matching lower bound, up to logarithmic factors, in the single-active-region case. Overall, the results show that even feedback-only shilling can sharply alter the statistical difficulty of repeated bidding.

</details>


### [201] [A Martingale Kernel Independence Test](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22549)
*Felix Laumann,Zhaolu Liu,Mauricio Barahona*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需置换检验的独立性测试方法（$m\mathrm{HSIC}$ 与 $md\mathrm{HSIC}$），利用鞅构建技术将检验统计量的极限分布标准化为标准正态分布，在保证精度的前提下极大提升了计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的 HSIC 和 $d\mathrm{HSIC}$ 作为退化 $V$-统计量，其零分布依赖于数据，必须使用置换检验进行校准，导致计算成本显著增加（通常增加两个数量级）。

Method: 借鉴两样本检验的鞅 MMD 构造思想，构建了两个学生化（studentised）统计量：$m\mathrm{HSIC}$（基于 Hadamard 积的自归一化下三角和）和 $md\mathrm{HSIC}$（结合单半样本划分以处理联合独立性测试）。由于这两个统计量在零假设下渐近服从标准正态分布，因此可以直接进行分位数查询，无需置换检验。

Result: 在合成数据实验中，两种新统计量在 1 到 500 维输入及 2 到 10 个联合变量的测试中，均能达到与传统置换检验相当的 I 类错误率和统计功效，且运行速度提升了 25 至 60 倍。

Conclusion: 本文提出的 $m\mathrm{HSIC}$ 和 $md\mathrm{HSIC}$ 统计量实现了渐近标准正态分布，从而完全消除了计算昂贵的置换检验需求，同时在保持统计功效的同时显著提升了计算效率（快 25-60 倍）。

Abstract: The Hilbert-Schmidt Independence Criterion (HSIC) and its joint-independence extension $d\mathrm{HSIC}$ are degenerate $V$-statistics whose data-dependent weighted-$χ^2$ null limits force a permutation calibration that multiplies the per-test cost by the number of permutations, in practice two orders of magnitude. Adapting the recent martingale MMD construction for two-sample testing to the (joint) independence problem, we introduce two studentised statistics whose null distributions are standard normal regardless of the data law, so that a single normal-quantile lookup replaces the permutation step entirely. The first, $m\mathrm{HSIC}$, is a self-normalised lower-triangular sum of the Hadamard product of two empirically centred Gram matrices. Under independence and bounded-fourth-moment kernels it converges to a standard normal. It is consistent against every fixed alternative, and runs at quadratic cost in the sample size without any sample split, matching the biased HSIC $V$-statistic. Our second statistic, $md\mathrm{HSIC}$, achieves finite-sample consistency with a single half-sample split: the centring is estimated on one half and the lower-triangular self-normalised martingale is run on the other, shrinking the conditional-mean residual to a quantity that is exponentially small in $d$, so the statistic is asymptotically standard normal at every fixed number of jointly tested variables, with a per-test cost that grows only linearly in $d$. On synthetic data with per-variable input dimension from $1$ to $500$ and between $2$ and $10$ jointly tested variables, both statistics match the empirical type-I error rate and test power of permutation-calibrated baselines while running $25$ to $60\times$ faster.

</details>


### [202] [Finite-Particle Convergence Rates for Conservative and Non-Conservative Drifting Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22795)
*Krishnakumar Balasubramanian*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用于一步生成建模的“保守漂移”方法，通过引入KDE梯度场规避了传统方法的非保守性，并从理论上证明了其有限粒子收敛速度及生成性能保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对现有的基于位移的一步生成漂移方法存在非保守性（non-conservatism）的问题，提出一种保守的漂移方法，以提高生成模型的稳定性和理论收敛性。

Method: 提出了一种改进的生成建模漂移方法：使用核密度估计（KDE）的梯度（即核平滑数据分数与模型分数的差）替代传统的位移速度。通过联合熵恒等式证明了连续时间有限粒子收敛界，并分析了该方法在不同求积正则性条件下的收敛率。同时，对Laplace核的非保守漂移方法进行了对比分析，并通过漂移大小的显式推导将其转化为一步生成保证。

Result: 证明了该保守漂移方法在 $\mathbb{R}^d$ 上的连续时间有限粒子收敛性。导出了明确的残差速度收敛率，在特定条件下可达到 $N^{-1/(d+4)}$ 或最优 $N^{-(2-\beta)/(2(d+4-\beta))}$ 的收敛率，并阐明了这些理论结果如何转化为实际的一步生成性能保证。

Conclusion: 该研究成功通过核密度估计（KDE）梯度场实现了一步生成建模的保守漂移，解决了传统基于位移漂移场非保守导致的问题，并提供了明确的有限粒子收敛速度界限，为一步生成提供了严谨的理论保证。

Abstract: We propose and analyze a conservative drifting method for one-step generative modeling. The method replaces the original displacement-based drifting velocity by a kernel density estimator (KDE)-gradient velocity, namely the difference of the kernel-smoothed data score and the kernel-smoothed model score. This velocity is a gradient field, addressing the non-conservatism issue identified for general displacement-based drifting fields. We prove continuous-time finite-particle convergence bounds for the conservative method on $\R^d$: a joint-entropy identity yields bounds for the empirical Stein drift, the smoothed Fisher discrepancy of the KDE, and the squared center velocity. The main finite-particle correction is a reciprocal-KDE self-interaction term, and we give deterministic and high-probability local-occupancy conditions under which this term is controlled. We keep the quadrature constants explicit and track their possible bandwidth dependence: the root residual-velocity rate $N^{-1/(d+4)}$ holds under an additional $h$-uniform quadrature regularity condition, while a more general growth condition yields the optimized root rate $N^{-(2-β)/(2(d+4-β))}$, where $0\le β<2$. We also analyze the non-conservative drifting method with Laplace kernel, corresponding to the original displacement-based velocity proposed in~\cite{deng2026drifting}. For this method, a sharp companion kernel decomposes the velocity into a positive scalar preconditioning of a sharp-score mismatch plus a Laplace scale-mismatch residual, producing an analogous finite-particle rate with an unavoidable residual term. Finally, we explain how the continuous-time residual-velocity bounds translate into one-step generation guarantees through the explicit drift size $η$.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [203] [AOP-Wiki EMOD 3.0: Data Model Expansions and Content Evaluation Framework for Using Agentic AI to Improve Integration between AOPs and New Approach Methodologies (NAMs)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21645)
*Virginia K. Hench,J. Harry Caufield,Sierra A. T. Moxon,Jason M. O'Brien,Stephen W. Edwards*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 AOP-Wiki EMOD 3.0 原型，旨在通过现代化数据模型和增强语义结构，克服当前 AOP-Wiki 的架构限制，以适配 AI 驱动的数据聚合，服务于未来的定量风险评估和计算 AOP 生成需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 AOP-Wiki 在数据模型和架构上存在局限性，难以支撑 AOP 领域的持续扩展、计算生成以及与生成式 AI 的深度融合。

Method: 开发了 AOP-Wiki EMOD 3.0 原型，引入了数据模型扩展，专注于提升数据结构化水平、增强 FAIR 性和 AI 就绪性，并改善了 AOP 框架与新方法 (NAMs) 的集成。

Result: 成功设计并论证了 AOP-Wiki EMOD 3.0 原型，证明了通过结构化证据和模型改进可有效支持 AOP 的现代化，以及其在监管科学、生物医学和“同一健康”（One Health）背景下的应用潜力。

Conclusion: AOP-Wiki EMOD 3.0 通过数据模型升级，提升了对计算化学和定量 AOP (qAOP) 的支持，增强了 FAIR 原则和 AI 就绪性，为下一代风险评估奠定了技术基础。

Abstract: Adverse Outcome Pathways (AOP) are logic models that causally link biological mechanisms that can be measured in a lab to adverse outcomes, relevant to chemical regulatory endpoints. AOPs contextualize new approach methodologies (NAMs), in vitro and in silico methods used as alternatives to animal testing and the sequential events in an AOP serve as multi-scale models spanning biological scales. The AOP-Wiki serves as the global repository for AOPs. While the AOP-Wiki has played a central role in AOP expansion over the past decade, constraints within the current data model and application infrastructure limit the AOP-Wiki from supporting continued AOP growth and evolution. Yet, the transformative power of agentic AI has re-invigorated AOP-Wiki data modernization efforts at a time when core AOP principles can be harnessed to inform use of AI for aggregating and structuring AOP-relevant information. Seizing upon this momentum, we present AOP-Wiki EMOD 3.0, the third in a series of evidence model prototypes, which concretely demonstrates data model expansions and our vision for how the AOP-Wiki might be transformed to better serve regulatory science and emergent use of AOPs in biomedical and One Health contexts. We aim to lay a foundation to support computationally-generated AOPs and quantitative AOPs (qAOPs) by focussing on solutions for AOP-Wiki internal quality improvement, evidence structuring to enhance AOP FAIRness and AI-readiness, and improved integration between the AOP framework and NAMs to better serve next generation risk assessment.

</details>


### [204] [Evaluation of Pipelines for Data Integration into Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22304)
*Marvin Hofer,Erhard Rahm*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了 KGI-Bench，这是一个用于评估知识图谱整合流水线质量的基准框架，包含评估指标和电影领域的基准数据集，旨在帮助确定最优的整合方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识图谱整合过程中存在多种流水线方案，但目前尚缺乏通用的方法来评估这些流水线的整体质量和性能，难以确定最佳整合策略。

Method: 提出了 KGI-Bench 基准测试框架，利用三个互补的质量指标（覆盖率、正确性、一致性）对知识图谱整合流水线的输出进行评估，并提供电影领域的基准数据集（种子图谱、多种输入格式数据、参考图谱）进行实验验证。

Result: 通过该框架对 12 个整合流水线进行了对比评估，展示了不同输入数据格式和设计选择对整合效果的影响。

Conclusion: KGI-Bench 为评估知识图谱整合流水线提供了系统性的方法和基准数据集，通过实验验证了其在对比不同设计选择时的有效性。

Abstract: Integrating new data into knowledge graphs (KG) typically involves different tasks that are executed within workflows or pipelines There are many possible pipelines for a specific integration problem but there is not yet a general approach to evaluate the overall quality and performance of such pipelines to be able to determine the best choices. We therefore propose a new benchmark KGI-Bench to evaluate integration pipelines that ingest different kinds of input data into an existing KG. We evaluate pipelines by analyzing their output, i.e., the updated KG, with the three complementary quality metrics coverage, correctness and consistency. We also provide benchmark datasets (seed KG, overlapping input data of three formats, reference KG as a ground truth) for the movie domain. To demonstrate the applicability and usefulness of the proposed benchmark, we comparatively evaluate 12 pipelines and analyze their behavior across different input data formats and design choices.

</details>


### [205] [Search-E1: Self-Distillation Drives Self-Evolution in Search-Augmented Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22511)
*Zihan Liang,Yufei Ma,Ben Chen,Zhipeng Qian,Xuxin Zhang,Huangyu Dai,Lingtao Mao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Search-E1 通过 GRPO 结合离线自蒸馏，简化了搜索增强 Agent 的训练流程，去除了复杂的外部强化机制，但在性能上超越了现有开源模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的搜索增强推理 Agent 依赖于复杂的额外机制（如过程奖励模型、树搜索、多阶段课程学习等），这不仅增加了训练管线的复杂度，还依赖于额外的资源或人工设计。探究是否能简化这些机制并仍保持或提升性能。

Method: 提出 Search-E1 方法：基于 GRPO（组相对策略优化）进行基础训练，并结合离线自蒸馏（Offline Self-Distillation, OFSD）。在每轮 GRPO 后，模型通过特权上下文（Privileged Context）识别更优的推理路径，利用 Token 级的 Forward KL 散度进行自蒸馏，从而获得稠密的单步监督信号。

Result: Search-E1 在七个问答基准测试上取得了 0.440 的平均精确匹配（EM）得分，使用 Qwen2.5-3B 模型即超越了所有同规模及更大规模的开源基线。

Conclusion: 研究表明，通过极简的自我进化方法（GRPO+OFSD）而非复杂的强化辅助机制，即可获得卓越的搜索增强推理性能。Search-E1证明了通过离线自蒸馏实现的高效路径对齐，足以替代多种外部监督和繁琐的技巧。

Abstract: Post-training has become the dominant recipe for turning a language model into a competent search-augmented reasoning agent. A line of recent work pushes its performance further by adding elaborate machinery on top of this standard pipeline. These augmentations import external supervision from stronger external systems, attach auxiliary modules such as process reward models or retrospective critics, restructure the rollout itself with tree search or multi-stage curricula, or shape the reward with hand-crafted bonuses and penalties. Each addition delivers a measurable gain, but each also inflates the training pipeline and ties the recipe to resources or designs that may not always be available. We take a step back and ask whether any of this machinery is actually necessary, and propose Search-E1, a self-evolution method that lets a search-augmented agent improve through only vanilla GRPO interleaved with offline self-distillation (OFSD). After each GRPO round, the policy rolls out on its own training questions. A token-level forward KL objective then aligns the policy's inference-time distribution to its own distribution under a privileged context that exposes a more efficient sibling trajectory. Despite this simplicity, the procedure naturally provides dense per-step supervision. On seven QA benchmarks, Search-E1 reaches $0.440$ average EM with Qwen2.5-3B, surpassing all open-source baselines at both scales. Code and complete version will be made public soon.

</details>


### [206] [Efficient Agentic Reasoning Through Self-Regulated Simulative Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22138)
*Mingkai Deng,Jinyu Hou,Lara Sá Neves,Varad Pimpalkhute,Taylor W. Killian,Zhengzhong Liu,Eric P. Xing*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出SR²AM架构，通过将智能体推理拆解为模拟推理、自我调节和反应式执行三个系统，实现了按需规划，在大幅降低推理成本的同时保持了极高的任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端训练的推理模型（如使用思维链）缺乏对规划过程的主动控制，导致推理长度冗余、推理效率低下，且未获得可靠的精度提升。

Method: 提出了SR²AM架构，将推理过程分解为三种系统：基于世界模型的模拟推理（System II）、基于学习配置器的自我调节（System III）以及反应式执行（System I）。该方法利用提示工程或从预训练轨迹中重构计划，并通过监督学习和强化学习（RL）进行训练。

Result: SR²AM模型在保持Pass@1性能与大规模模型（120B-1T参数）相当的同时，推理令牌消耗减少了25.8%-95.3%。RL训练使得模型能够有效延长规划视野（+22.8%）而非频繁无效规划（仅增长2.0%）。

Conclusion: 引入自我调节机制（System III）能够有效优化大模型的推理行为，使其在任务执行中学会“何时”及“如何”进行深层规划，从而在显著降低计算成本的同时保持甚至超越大规模模型的性能。

Abstract: How should an agent decide when and how to plan? A dominant approach builds agents as reactive policies with adaptive computation (e.g., chain-of-thought), trained end-to-end expecting planning to emerge implicitly. Without control over the presence, structure, or horizon of planning, these systems dramatically increase reasoning length, yielding inefficient token use without reliable accuracy gains. We argue efficient agentic reasoning benefits from decomposing decision-making into three systems: simulative reasoning (System II) grounding deliberation in future-state prediction via a world model; self-regulation (System III) deciding when and how deeply to plan via a learned configurator; and reactive execution (System I) handling fine-grained action. Simulative reasoning provides unified planning across diverse tasks without per-domain engineering, while self-regulation ensures the planner is invoked only when needed. To test this, we develop SR$^2$AM (Self-Regulated Simulative Reasoning Agentic LLM), realizing both as distinct stages within an LLM's chain-of-thought, with the LLM as world model. We explore two instantiations: recording decisions from a prompted multi-module system (v0.1) and reconstructing structured plans from traces of pretrained reasoning LLMs (v1.0), trained via supervised then reinforcement learning (RL). Across math, science, tabular analysis, and web information seeking, v0.1-8B and v1.0-30B achieve Pass@1 competitive with 120-355B and 685B-1T parameter systems respectively, while v1.0-30B uses 25.8-95.3% fewer reasoning tokens than comparable agentic LLMs. RL increases average planning horizon by 22.8% while planning frequency grows only 2.0%, showing it learns to plan further ahead rather than more often. More broadly, learned self-regulation instantiates a principle we expect to extend beyond planning to how agents govern their own learning and adaptation.

</details>


### [207] [Ratchet: A Minimal Hygiene Recipe for Self-Evolving LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22148)
*Xing Zhang,Yanwei Cui,Guanghui Wang,Ziyuan Li,Wei Qiu,Bing Zhu,Peiyang He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对LLM Agent自进化技能库无效的问题，提出Ratchet框架，引入技能自动化生命周期管理，显著提升了Agent在复杂编程任务中的长期性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前研究（如Voyager）虽能积累技能，但LLM自主编写的技能往往无法带来性能提升。研究发现瓶颈不在于技能生成，而在于缺乏有效的技能生命周期管理。

Method: 提出Ratchet单Agent循环框架，包含四种技能卫生机制：基于结果的技能退休（outcome-driven retirement）、有界活跃技能上限（bounded active-cap）、元技能编写指导（meta-skill authoring guidance）以及模式规范化（pattern canonicalisation）。

Result: 在MBPP+任务上将pass@1从0.258提升至0.584（峰值0.658）；在SWE-bench Verified任务上实现+0.22的峰值性能提升，并论证了该机制的非发散性（防止性能退化）。

Conclusion: Ratchet通过引入技能生命周期管理（退休机制、容量限制、元技能指导等），有效解决了大模型自进化技能库中冗余和低质量技能堆积的问题，显著提升了Agent在复杂任务（如MBPP+和SWE-bench）中的性能。

Abstract: Self-evolving skill libraries, pioneered by Voyager, let frozen LLM agents accumulate reusable knowledge without weight updates, yet recent evaluation shows that LLM-authored skills deliver $+0.0$pp over no-skill baselines while human-curated ones deliver $+16.2$pp: the bottleneck is not skill authoring but lifecycle management. We introduce \textbf{Ratchet}, a single-agent loop in which a frozen LLM writes, retrieves, curates, and retires its own natural-language skills. Ratchet integrates four candidate hygiene mechanisms: outcome-driven retirement, a bounded active-cap, meta-skill authoring guidance, and pattern canonicalisation. On MBPP+ hard-100 with Claude Opus 4.7, Ratchet lifts held-out pass@1 from a $0.258 \pm 0.047$ baseline to a late-window rolling mean of $0.584$ (peak $0.658 \pm 0.042$) across 100 rounds and 3 seeds, a $+0.328 \pm 0.018$ rolling-mean gain where the no-skill control drifts at $+0.002 \pm 0.005$; the same recipe transfers to an agentic solver on SWE-bench Verified ($+0.22$ peak lift over 20 rounds). Eight ablations (A1--A8) reveal that the minimal working recipe is smaller than our design suggests: retirement and the meta-skill authoring prior are load-bearing, while explicit deduplication (canonicalisation, cover-guard) is subsumed by the meta-skill itself. A non-divergence proposition shows that bounded cap and retirement threshold together prevent expected performance from drifting below the no-skills floor.

</details>


### [208] [Epicure: Navigating the Emergent Geometry of Food Ingredient Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22391)
*Jakub Radzikowski,Josef Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Epicure系列多语言食谱成分嵌入模型，通过结合共现统计与化学属性，在多语言环境下实现了对食品成分的精准表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有食谱嵌入往往受限于数据规模或单一维度（仅统计共现或仅基于化学组成），缺乏能够同时结合烹饪语境与化学属性的通用成分表示模型。

Method: 1. 构建多语言食谱数据集（4.14M条/9种语言），并通过LLM管道归一化至1,790个 canonical 条目；2. 建立成分-成分NPMI图和成分-化合物图；3. 利用Metapath2Vec算法，根据三种不同的随机游走模式（Cooc, Chem, Core）训练三种变体嵌入。

Result: 成功构建了包含不同语义侧重点的模型家族（Epicure系列），能够捕捉不同层面的成分间关系（化学相似性与烹饪搭配性）。

Conclusion: Epicure提供了一组从多语言食谱语料库中提取的成分嵌入模型，通过结合化学成分信息（Chem）和共现关系（Cooc），在揭示成分关联性方面优于单一维度模型。

Abstract: We present Epicure, a family of three sibling skip-gram ingredient embeddings retrained from scratch on a multilingual recipe corpus. We aggregate 4.14M recipes from 11 sources spanning seven languages, English, Chinese, Russian, Vietnamese, Spanish, Turkish, Indonesian, German, and Indian-English, and normalise the raw ingredient strings to 1,790 canonical entries via an LLM-augmented pipeline. A 203,508-edge ingredient-ingredient NPMI graph and an 80,019-edge typed FlavorDB ingredient-compound graph, 2,247 typed compound nodes across 15 categories, seed three Metapath2Vec variants that share architecture and hyperparameters and differ only in the random-walk schema: Cooc walks the co-occurrence graph only, Chem walks the typed compound metapaths only, and Core blends both via injected ingredient-ingredient walks at controlled mixing, placing each model at a distinct point on the chemistry-vs-recipe-context spectrum.

</details>


### [209] [AMEL: Accumulated Message Effects on LLM Judgments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22714)
*Sid-ali Temkit*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现大模型作为自动评估器时存在“累积消息效应（AMEL）”，即历史对话会造成后续打分偏置。该效应与历史长度无关，但呈现负面偏置更强的特性，且单纯依靠模型规模提升难以彻底解决，建议通过重置上下文或平衡历史记录来规避。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨大语言模型（LLM）作为自动化评估工具时，先前的对话历史是否会对其后续评估结果产生偏置影响，并量化该效应的特征。

Method: 通过75,898次API调用，对比11种主流模型在“孤立评估”与“饱和正/负面历史”评估下的差异，分析了模型熵值、历史长度、偏见不对称性（负面偏见更强）及模型规模对该效应的影响。

Result: 1. 存在显著偏置（d=-0.17），对不确定性（高熵）项目影响更大（d=-0.34）；2. 偏置强度与上下文长度无关；3. 存在“消极不对称性”，负面历史产生的偏置能力是正面历史的1.62倍；4. 模型扩展（Scale）无法完全消除此效应。

Conclusion: LLM在评估任务中存在显著的累积消息效应（AMEL），即先前的对话历史会偏置后续的判断。通过重置上下文或平衡历史记录可以有效缓解该问题。

Abstract: Large language models are routinely used as automated evaluators: to review code, moderate content, or score outputs, often with many items passing through one conversation. We ask whether the polarity of prior conversation history biases subsequent judgments, an effect we call the accumulated message effect on LLM judgments (AMEL). Across 75,898 API calls to 11 models from 4 providers (OpenAI, Anthropic, Google, and four open-source models), we present identical test items in isolation or following histories saturated with predominantly positive or negative evaluations.
  Models shift toward the conversation's prevailing polarity (d = -0.17, p < 10^-46). The effect concentrates on items where the model is genuinely uncertain at baseline (d = -0.34 for high-entropy items, vs d = -0.15 when the baseline is deterministic). Bias does not grow with context length: 5 prior turns and 50 produce the same shift (Spearman |r| < 0.01; OLS slope p = 0.80). And there is a negativity asymmetry: paired per item, negative histories induce 1.62x more bias than positive (t = 13.46, p < 10^-39, n = 2,481). Scaling helps but does not solve it (Anthropic: Haiku -0.22 to Opus -0.17; OpenAI: Nano -0.34 to GPT-5.2 -0.17).
  Three follow-ups narrow the mechanism. The token probability distribution shifts continuously, not at a threshold. The negativity asymmetry has both token-level and semantic components, though attributing the balance is exploratory at our sample sizes. Position does not matter: five biased turns anywhere in a 50-turn history produce the same shift. The simplest fix for evaluation pipelines is a fresh context per item; when batching is unavoidable, balancing the history helps.

</details>


### [210] [Skill Weaving: Efficient LLM Improvement via Modular Skillpacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22205)
*Zhuo Li,Guodong Du,Zesheng Shi,Weiyang Guo,Weijun Yao,Yuan Zhou,Jiabo Zhang,Jing Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SkillWeave 是一种通过模块化技能包和压缩技术实现的大模型专用化框架，能在保持低资源占用的前提下，显著提升多领域任务性能并降低推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大模型在满足多领域专用化需求时，面临内存占用大和推理延迟高的矛盾。

Method: 提出 SkillWeave 框架：将大模型能力划分为轻量级的“技能包”（skillpacks）；引入 SkillZip 技术对这些技能包进行压缩，以实现低延迟推理。

Result: 一个 9B 的 SkillWeave 模型在多任务与智能体基准测试中，性能优于多个基准模型，甚至超过了 32B 的单体模型，且推理速度提升高达 4 倍。

Conclusion: SkillWeave 提供了一种在固定内存预算下实现大模型领域专用化的有效路径，通过模块化和压缩技术平衡了性能与效率。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly require specialization across diverse domains, yet existing approaches struggle to balance multi-domain capacities with strict memory and inference constraints. In this work, we introduce SkillWeave, a modular improvement framework that enables LLMs to specialize under fixed memory budgets. SkillWeave partitions full capabilities of a general-purpose model into skillpacks -- lightweight, domain-specific delta modules -- that reorganize and refine the model's internal knowledge. For efficient deployment, SkillWeave integrates SkillZip to compress skillpacks into compact and inference-ready format, enabling strong multi-domain performance with low-latency execution. On multi-task and agentic benchmarks, a 9B SkillWeave model outperforms several baselines and even surpasses a 32B monolithic LLM, while achieving up to 4x speedup.

</details>


### [211] [Cross-domain benchmarks reveal when coordinated AI agents improve scientific inference from partial evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22300)
*Fiona Y. Wong,Markus J. Buehler*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个跨科学领域的基准测试，评估了在何种条件下协调式AI智能体能比简单工作流提供更高的价值，并发现其价值取决于数据的互补性、任务的解释需求以及与基线的对比表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学数据分散在不同仪器、数据库和学科中，单一来源难以覆盖完整现象。因此，需要明确在何种情况下，协调式AI智能体对比简单的科学工作流能提供真正的价值增益。

Method: 构建了一个涵盖四个科学任务（分子结构音乐化映射、科学范式转移检测、媒介传播疾病识别、系外行星候选者筛查）的跨领域基准测试；使用冻结评分面板、预定义的打分协议、明确的基线对比以及消融实验进行评估。

Result: 在跨领域任务中，当学科信息具互补性时，跨通道组合优于单通道基线（如媒介疾病识别AUROC 0.944，系外行星筛查AUROC 0.955）；但在某些任务中，分解式工作流并不比强效的汇总基线更优；在信号明确的任务中，智能体的主要价值在于提升解释性与可追溯性。

Conclusion: 研究定义了AI智能体辅助科学工作流的三种运行机制：跨通道整合（当多学科信息互补时）、效能对比（当单一基准足够强大时）以及解释与表征优化（当主导信号明确时）。结论认为，仅在有明确基准支持且性能、可追溯性或表征确实得到提升的情况下，协调式AI智能体才具有实际价值。

Abstract: Scientific evidence often spans instruments, databases, and disciplines, so no single source records the full phenomenon. This makes it difficult to determine when coordinated AI agents add value over simpler scientific workflows. We evaluate this question with a cross-domain benchmark spanning four scientific tasks: mapping molecular structure into musical representations, detecting historical paradigm shifts in science, identifying vector-borne disease emergence, and vetting transiting-exoplanet candidates. Each case uses a frozen evaluation panel, predefined scoring protocols, explicit baselines, ablations or null controls, and stated limitations. The results define three operating regimes. When different disciplines each capture only part of the phenomenon, cross-channel composites improve over single-channel baselines: climate-vector emergence reaches AUROC 0.944 and exoplanet vetting reaches AUROC 0.955. However, the exoplanet workflow is effectively tied with a strong combined-summary baseline, showing that decomposition does not always improve top-line performance. When one signal dominates, as in paradigm-shift detection, coordination mainly improves interpretation and traceability. For molecular sonification, the gain is representational rather than predictive. ScienceClaw x Infinite provides the auditable artifact and provenance layer for this evaluation. The benchmark therefore assigns value to coordination only when the corresponding performance, provenance, or representation claim is supported by explicit comparators.

</details>


<div id='cs.PF'></div>

### [212] [Throughput-Optimal Multiresource-Job Scheduling with Continuous Requirement Distribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21715)
*Heyuan Yao,Willow Kowalik,Izzy Grosof*

Main category: cs.PF

TL;DR: 本文针对现代计算系统中资源需求呈连续分布的特点，提出了首个适用于连续多资源作业模型的吞吐量最优调度策略，并通过离散化方法提升了计算效率，在Google Borg数据集中表现优于现有最优策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于队列稳定性和吞吐量最优的理论研究多基于“类”模型，要求资源需求种类较少，但现代计算系统中作业资源需求极其多样化，呈连续分布，导致现有模型失效。

Method: 提出了针对连续多资源作业（MRJ）模型的吞吐量最优调度策略族（包括可抢占式和不可抢占式）；开发了基于负载和需求分布的离散化方法以提高计算效率；并与现有的索引策略进行了性能对比。

Result: 证明了所提策略的吞吐量最优性；实验表明该方法在参数化分布和Google Borg调度器轨迹数据上均实现了最先进（SOTA）的性能。

Conclusion: 该研究成功提出了适用于连续资源需求模型的吞吐量最优调度策略，弥补了现有基于类（class-based）模型在处理多样化资源需求时的不足，并验证了其在实际数据中心场景中的表现。

Abstract: Modern computing systems process jobs with resource requirements such as CPU and memory, which are described by multiresource jobs (MRJ) queueing models. In practice, job resource requirements are spread out over so many values, that it is rare to see the same value twice. This pattern is best modeled by a continuous distribution of requirement values. However, the existing theoretical work on stability or throughput-optimality focuses on queueing models with class-based resource requirements. In class-based models, the number of distinct resource requirements must be small to demonstrate strong empirical performance, making them a poor match for these practical systems.
  We introduce the first throughput-optimal family of scheduling policies for the continuous MRJ model, with both preemptive and nonpreemptive variants. We further introduce several efficient policy families, which remain throughput-optimal while considerably improving computational efficiency, under some distributional assumptions. We use a discretization approach, where we choose the discretization granularity based on the system load and the distribution of resource requirements. We validate the real-world applicability of our policies by comparing them against existing index-based policies on parametrized distributions and on datacenter trace data from the Google Borg scheduler, demonstrating state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [213] [DynaFlow: Transparent and Flexible Intra-Device Parallelism via Programmable Operator Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21603)
*Yi Pan,Yile Gu,Jinbin Luo,Yibo Wu,Ziren Wang,Hongtao Zhang,Ziyi Xu,Shengkai Lin,Baris Kasikci,Stephanie Wang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: DynaFlow 是一个旨在解决 ML 推理与训练中设备内并行实现难、集成成本高问题的框架，通过解耦逻辑定义与物理调度，实现透明且灵活的并行化，并取得显著的吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ML 系统中设备内并行（Intra-device parallelism）虽能提升资源利用率，但受限于现有框架静态、序列化的模型定义模式，导致手动集成成本高昂，且策略高度依赖执行环境，扩展性差。

Method: 提出一套包含动态前端与高效后端的框架：(1) 前端：引入图划分标注及自定义并行策略的可编程接口；(2) 后端：管理异步控制/数据流，采用自定义内存管理消除拷贝开销，并兼容 CUDA Graphs 和 TorchInductor 等现有优化技术。

Result: DynaFlow 成功将多种代表性并行策略集成至 6 个前沿 ML 系统中，且仅需少量代码修改，最高实现 1.29 倍的吞吐量提升。

Conclusion: DynaFlow 通过解耦逻辑模型定义与物理执行调度，有效解决了并行策略集成成本高、上下文敏感度高等挑战。该框架实现了透明的并行化集成，既提升了硬件资源利用率，又保持了对现有深度学习优化技术的兼容性，为复杂 ML 系统提供了一个易用且高效的优化途径。

Abstract: Intra-device parallelism addresses resource under-utilization in ML inference and training by overlapping the execution of operators with different resource usage. However, its wide adoption is hindered by a fundamental conflict with the static, sequential programming model of existing frameworks. Integrating these strategies requires invasive, model-specific code overhauls, representing an intractable engineering cost. This is further amplified by the high sensitivity of strategies to execution contexts (e.g., workload, model architecture, hardware), forcing developers to implement and maintain multiple specialized solutions. To address this, we propose DynaFlow, a framework that enables the transparent and flexible integration of intra-device parallelism by decoupling the logical model definition from the physical execution schedule. DynaFlow introduces a flexible frontend with annotations for graph partitioning and a programmable interface for defining custom intra-device parallelism strategies. Its efficient backend manages complex control/data-flow asynchronously, uses custom memory management to eliminate copy overheads, and preserves compatibility with optimizations like CUDA Graphs and TorchInductor. We demonstrate that DynaFlow can integrate representative parallelism strategies into 6 state-of-the-art ML systems with minimal code changes, achieving up to a 1.29x throughput improvement. DynaFlow is publicly available at https://github.com/uw-syfi/DynaFlow.

</details>


### [214] [Secure and Parallel Determinant Computation for Large-Scale Matrices in Edge Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22039)
*Prajwal Panth*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种安全的并行行列式计算（SPDC）框架，通过 CED 加密和并行验证算法，在保障隐私安全的同时，解决了 IoT 边缘场景下行列式计算的性能瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 IoT 边缘计算场景中，矩阵行列式计算（MDC）计算量大，传统算法难以满足实时性要求，同时在不可信服务器上处理敏感数据存在隐私与安全隐患。

Method: 1. 提出复合元素扰动 (CED) 技术（含 EWO 和 PRT）实现数据隐私化；2. 基于并行 LU 分解将矩阵任务分布至多个边缘服务器；3. 采用单向通信模型降低协调开销；4. 设计 Q2（概率性）和 Q3（确定性）两种高效结果验证算法。

Result: 数学分析表明，该框架在保证强隐私保护和结果完整性的前提下，显著降低了计算与通信开销，提升了边缘计算的可扩展性。

Conclusion: 提出了一种高效且安全的边缘计算框架 SPDC，成功平衡了计算复杂度、隐私保护和验证安全性，非常适用于资源受限的 IoT 环境。

Abstract: The advent of edge computing has enabled resource-constrained clients to delegate intensive computational tasks to distributed edge servers, especially within Internet of Things (IoT) environments. Among such tasks, Matrix Determinant Computation (MDC) remains critical for applications in control systems, cryptography, and machine learning. However, the cubic complexity of traditional determinant algorithms makes them unsuitable for real-time processing in constrained edge scenarios.
  We propose a Secure Parallel Determinant Computation (SPDC) framework, which provides strong security guaranties, including privacy-preserving MDC, across N distributed edge servers. The framework achieves privacy through Composite Element Distortion (CED) - a lightweight encryption method that combines Element-wise Obfuscation (EWO) and the Panth Rotation Theorem (PRT) to conceal both structural and numerical matrix content while preserving determinant properties. Parallel LU decomposition is used to distribute encrypted matrix blocks across an arbitrary number of untrusted edge servers, enabling efficient and scalable determinant computation. A one-way communication model further reduces coordination overhead by eliminating inter-server interactions. To ensure result integrity with minimal client burden, we further introduce two verification algorithms: Q_2, a probabilistic scalar method, and Q_3, a deterministic and low-complexity alternative.
  Mathematical analysis demonstrates that the proposed framework provides strong privacy and security guaranties, low computational overhead, and deployment flexibility - making it well-suited for secure, scalable, and real-time MDC in distributed edge-assisted systems.

</details>


### [215] [Nf-PEAK: Process-Based Energy Attribution for Nextflow Workflows on Kubernetes Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22393)
*Philipp Thamm,Somayeh Mohammadi,Kathleen West,Knut Reinert,Lauritz Thamsen,Ulf Leser*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了 Nf-PEAK，一种能将 Kubernetes 节点级能耗精准拆解到任务级的分析方法，解决了共享集群中任务能耗监测难、受噪声干扰大的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学工作流在 Kubernetes 上的资源异构性和共享集群的限制（如 Intel RAPL 的节点级限制、权限访问受限、混部带来的测量噪声）使得难以精确评估单个任务的能耗。

Method: 1. 识别工作流节点（pods）；2. 通过 cgroup 元数据将 pods 映射到宿主机进程；3. 对 RAPL 计数器和进程级性能计数器进行采样；4. 构建并应用非线性“能量信贷”模型，将能耗分配至各个任务。

Result: 在隔离环境下平均绝对百分比误差（MAPE）为 6.6%，在存在负载干扰下为 10.9%；实验证明该方法在多节点扩展下表现稳定，且相比 Kepler 在高负载场景下具有更低的估算误差。

Conclusion: Nf-PEAK 是一种高效的容器化方法，能够在复杂的共享 Kubernetes 环境下提供准确的任务级能耗估算。相较于当前主流工具（如 Kepler），其在应对混部负载时的表现更为优越。

Abstract: Scientific workflows are pipelines of interdependent tasks. They are increasingly executed on shared Kubernetes clusters via workflow engines such as Nextflow. Their energy consumption matters for both cost and sustainability. It is necessary to examine and optimize workflow tasks individually, because they can be very heterogeneous. However, estimating task-level energy on clusters is difficult: Intel RAPL counters report only node-level energy, access to counters and host process information is typically restricted, and concurrent workloads introduce resource contention and measurement noise. We present Nf-PEAK, a containerized method to attribute CPU-package and DRAM energy to individual processes and Nextflow tasks. Nf-PEAK (i) identifies workflow pods, (ii) maps pods to host processes via cgroup metadata, (iii) samples RAPL and per-process performance counters, and (iv) applies a non-linear energy-credit model before aggregating results at task level. On a Kubernetes cluster, we evaluate three nf-core workflows under controlled co-located CPU load. Nf-PEAK reaches an average Mean Absolute Percentage Error of 6.6% in isolated runs and 10.9% when an unrelated workload saturates 8 of 32 hardware threads per node, and remains stable across 2, 3, 4, and 8 nodes. Compared to the state-of-the-art Kubernetes tool Kepler, Nf-PEAK yields lower error on average, particularly under co-located load.

</details>


### [216] [AI-Driven Multi-Region Provisioning for Cloud Services Using Spot Fleets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22778)
*Javier Fabra,Enrique Molina-Giménez,Pedro García-López*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于AWS多区域竞价实例的AI驱动供应框架，通过预测模型优化跨区域配置，在保持服务稳定性的前提下，显著降低了云计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对AWS EC2 Spot Service在多区域部署和成本预估方面的局限性，旨在解决竞价实例的价格波动、资源可用性及中断风险带来的不确定性，以实现更具经济效益的动态工作负载支撑。

Method: 提出了一种AI驱动的多区域竞价实例供应服务，结合了供应计划监控（monitoring of provisioning plans）与预测模型，在部署前对竞价实例的配置和价格进行估算，并支持跨区域的成本感知部署决策。

Result: 在包含高达1500个vCPU的集群实验中，该系统的预测准确性达到了99.79%，相较于单区域部署，通过利用多区域价格差异，成本最多可降低64%。

Conclusion: 该AI驱动的供应服务能够有效在跨区域环境中利用竞价实例（Spot Instances），在维持EC2 Spot Service原生行为的同时，实现了显著的成本优化（高达64%）和高精度的成本预测（准确率99.79%）。

Abstract: Cloud service platforms increasingly rely on elastic infrastructures to support dynamic workloads. Spot instances provide discounted computing resources but introduce uncertainty due to dynamic pricing, resource availability, and interruption risks that vary across geographical regions. In Amazon Web Services, the EC2 Spot Service simplifies fleet provisioning through allocation strategies, but it cannot estimate fleet costs before deployment and restricts provisioning to a single region. This paper presents an AI-driven provisioning service for multi-region spot fleets. The proposed approach combines monitoring of provisioning plans with predictive models to estimate fleet configurations and prices before launch, enabling cost-aware deployment decisions across regions while preserving the operational behavior of the EC2 Spot Service. The system was validated with fleets of up to 1500 vCPUs. Experimental results show a prediction accuracy of 99.79% compared to the EC2 Spot Service and potential cost savings of up to 64% by exploiting regional price variability.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [217] [CompPow: A Case for Component-level GPU Power Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21847)
*Shaizeen Aga,Mohamed Assem Ibrahim*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出CompPow架构，通过实现GPU组件级能耗感知管理，在提升ML任务能效的同时优化了系统处理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ML驱动的智能应用导致GPU普及，但GPU功耗极大；现有的优化多关注数据中心整体，缺乏对GPU内部组件功耗机制的细粒度研究。

Method: 提出“CompPow”框架，通过赋予GPU组件级感知能力（component-awareness），精细化管理GPU内部不同组件的功耗。

Result: 在多种ML操作和执行模式下，CompPow验证了可实现10%的能效提升，并带来5%的性能改进。

Conclusion: 通过组件感知的软硬件协同设计，可以进一步挖掘现代GPU的能效潜力，为未来的节能计算提供指导。

Abstract: The ever increasing demand for ML-driven intelligence in a wide spectrum of domains has led to ubiquity of GPUs. At the same time, GPUs are notorious for their power consumption needs and often dominate power allocation in a typical ML datacenter. While datacenter-level power optimizations which focus on collection of GPUs are promising, in this work, we take a different tack -- namely, we take a closer look at power consumption inside a GPU. Specifically, as modern GPUs are comprised of integrated components, we make a case for component-awareness, termed CompPow in this work, for improved power management in modern GPUs. We demonstrate for a variety of ML operations and execution patterns, CompPow has the potential to deliver higher energy efficiency (10%) and even improved performance (5%). We conclude with recommendations on how component-aware software-hardware co-design can extract additional energy efficiency from modern GPUs.
